# Petavatthu

> **Nikāya:** Khuddakanikāya
> **Book:** Petavatthu
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

---


<!-- PTS Page 001 -->
<a id="page-001"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 001]*

<p align="center">Namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa.</p>


1 URAGAVAGGA I-  URAGAVAGGA 1 Khettūpamapetavatthu[^001-1] Vv_I,1[=1].1    Khettūpamā arahanto dāyakā kassakūpamā bījūpamaṃ deyyadhammaṃ etto[^001-2] nibbattate phalaṃ.  || 1 ||  
Vv_I,1[=1].2    Etaṃ[^001-3] bījaṃ kasī[^001-4] khettaṃ petānaṃ dāyakassa ca, taṃ petā paribhuñjanti dātā puññena vaḍḍhati.  || 2 ||  
Vv_I,1[=1].3    Idh'; eva kusalaṃ katvā pete ca paṭipūjiya[^001-5] saggañ ca kamati[^001-6] ṭṭhānaṃ[^001-7] kammaṃ katvāna bhaddakan ti.  || 3 ||  
khettūpamapetavatthu paṭhamaṃ[^001-8]


<!-- PTS Page 002 -->
<a id="page-002"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 002]*

2 Sūkarapetavatthu Vv_I,2[=2].1    Kāyo te sabbasovaṇṇo[^002-1] sabbā obhāsate disā mukhaṃ te sūkarass'; eva kiṃ kammam akarī[^002-2] pure ti.  || 4 ||  
Vv_I,2[=2].2    Kāyena saññato āsiṃ vācāyāsim[^002-3] asaññato tena me tādiso vaṇṇo yathā passasi Nārada.[^002-4]  || 5 ||  
Vv_I,2[=2].3    Taṃ tyāhaṃ[^002-5] Nārada brūmi sāmaṃ diṭṭham idaṃ tayā mākāsi mukhasā pāpaṃ mā kho sūkaramukho ahū ti.  || 6 ||  
Sūkaramukhapetavatthu dutiyaṃ &1 3 Pūtimukhapetavatthu Vv_I,3[=3].1        [^002-1]Dibbaṃ subhaṃ dhāresi vaṇṇadhātuṃ vehāyasaṃ[^002-2] tiṭṭhasi antalikkhe[^002-3] mukhañ ca te kimayo[^002-4] pūtigandhaṃ khādanti, kiṃ kammam akāsi pubbe ti.  || 7 ||  
Vv_I,3[=3].2        [^002-1]Samaṇo ahaṃ pāpo 'tiduṭṭhavāco[^002-5] tapassirūpo[^002-6] mukhasā asaññato laddhā ca me tapasā vaṇṇadhātu[^002-7] mukhañ ca me pesuṇiyena[^002-8] pūti.[^002-9]  || 8 ||  
Vv_I,3[=3].3        Ta-y-idaṃ tayā Nārada sāmaṃ[^002-10] diṭṭhaṃ anukampakā ye kusalā vadeyyuṃ: mā pesuṇaṃ mā ca musā abhāṇi yakkho tuvaṃ hohisi kāmakāmī ti.  || 9 ||  
Pūtimukhapetavatthu tatiyaṃ &2


<!-- PTS Page 003 -->
<a id="page-003"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 003]*

4 Piṭṭhadhītalikapetavatthu Vv_I,4[=4].1    Yaṃ kiñc'; ārammaṇaṃ katvā dajjā dānaṃ amaccharī pubbapete[^003-1] ca[^003-2] ārabbha atha vā vatthudevatā;  || 10 ||  
Vv_I,4[=4].2    [^003-3]Cattāro ca[^003-2] mahārāje lokapāle yasassino[^003-4] Kuveraṃ Dhataraṭṭhañ ca Virūpakkhaṃ[^003-5] Virūḷhakaṃte[^003-6] c'; eva[^003-6] pūjitā honti dāyakā ca anipphalā.  || 11 ||  
Vv_I,4[=4].3    Na hi ruṇṇaṃ va[^003-7] soko vā yā c'; aññā paridevanā na taṃ petassa[^003-8] atthāya evaṃ tiṭṭhanti ñātayo.  || 12 ||  
Vv_I,4[=4].4    Ayañ ca kho dakkhiṇā dinnā saṅghamhi suppatiṭṭhitā dīgharattaṃ hitāy'; assa ṭhānaso upakappatī ti.  || 13 ||  
Piṭṭhadhītalikapetavatthu catutthaṃ &1 5 Tirokuḍḍapetavatthu Vv_I,5[=5].1    Tirokuḍḍesu[^003-1] tiṭṭhanti sandhisiṅghāṭakesu ca dvārabāhāsu tiṭṭhanti āgantvāna sakaṃ gharaṃ.  || 14 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].2   Pahūte annapānamhi khajjabhojje upaṭṭhite na tesaṃ koci sarati sattānaṃ kammapaccayā.  ||  
15 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 004 -->
<a id="page-004"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 004]*

Vv_I,5[=5].3    [^004-2]Evaṃ dadanti ñātīnaṃ ye honti anukampakā suciṃ paṇītaṃ kālena kappiyaṃ pānabhojanaṃ: idaṃ vo ñātīnaṃ[^004-3] hotu sukhitā hontu ñātayo.  || 16 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].4    Te ca tattha samāgantvā ñātipetā samāgatā pahūte annapānamhi sakkaccaṃ anumodare:  || 17 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].5    Ciraṃ jīvantu no ñātī yesaṃ hetu labhāmase amhākañ ca katā pūjā dāyakā ca anipphalā.  || 18 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].6    [^004-4]Na hi tattha kasī atthi gorakkh'; ettha[^004-5] na vijjati vaṇijjā tādisī natthi hiraññena[^004-6] kayakkayaṃ,[^004-7] ito dinnena yāpenti petā kālakatā[^004-8] tahiṃ.  || 19 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].7    Unname udakaṃ[^004-9] vuṭṭhaṃ[^004-10] yathā ninnaṃ pavattati evam eva ito dinnaṃ petānaṃ upakappati.  || 20 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].8    Yathā vārivahā pūrā paripūrenti sāgaraṃ evam eva ito dinnaṃ petānaṃ upakappati.  || 21 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].9    Adāsi me akāsi me ñātimittā sakhā ca me petānaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ[^004-11] dajjā pubbe katam anussaraṃ.  || 22 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].10   Na hi ruṇṇaṃ va[^004-12] soko vā yā c'; aññā paridevanā na taṃ petānam[^004-13] atthāya evaṃ tiṭṭhanti ñātayo.  || 23 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].11   Ayañ ca kho[^004-14] dakkhiṇā dinnā saṅghamhi[^004-15] suppatiṭṭhitā dīgharattaṃ hitāy'; assa ṭhānaso upakappati.  || 24 ||  
Vv_I,5[=5].12       So ñātidhammo ca ayaṃ nidassito petānaṃ pūjā ca katā uḷārā balañ ca bhikkhūnam anuppadinnaṃ tumhehi puññaṃ pasutaṃ anappakan ti.  || 25 ||  
Tirokuḍḍapetavatthu pañcamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 005 -->
<a id="page-005"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 005]*

6 Pañcaputtakhādakapetavatthu Vv_I,6[=6].1    Naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi duggandhā pūti vāyasi makkhikāparikiṇṇā[^005-1] va[^005-1] kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasī ti.  || 26 ||  
Vv_I,6[=6].2    Ahaṃ bhadante[^005-2] petī 'mhi duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokaṃ[^005-3] ito gatā.  || 27 ||  
Vv_I,6[=6].3    Kālena pañca puttāni sāyaṃ pañca punāpare vijāyitvāna khādāmi te pi nā[^005-4] honti me alaṃ.  || 28 ||  
Vv_I,6[=6].4    Pariḍayhati dhūmāyati khudāya[^005-5] hadayaṃ mama pānīyaṃ na labhe pātum passa mam vyasanam gatan ti.  || 29 ||  
Vv_I,6[=6].5    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ[^005-6] kataṃ[^005-7] kissa kammavipākena puttamaṃsāni khādasī ti.  || 30 ||  
Vv_I,6[=6].6    Sapattī[^005-8] me gabbhinī āsi tassā pāpaṃ acetayiṃ sāhaṃ paduṭṭhamanasā akariṃ gabbhapātanaṃ.  || 31 ||  
Vv_I,6[=6].7    Tassā dvemāsiko gabbho lohitañ ñeva paggharī[^005-9] tad'; assā mātā kupitā mayhaṃ ñātī samānayi, [^005-10]sapathañ ca maṃ kāresi paribhāsāpayī[^005-11] ca maṃ.  || 32 ||  
Vv_I,6[=6].8    Sāhaṃ ghorañ ca sapathaṃ musāvādaṃ abhāsisaṃ:[^005-12] puttamaṃsāni khādāmi sace[^005-13] taṃ pakataṃ[^005-13] mayā.  || 33 ||  
Vv_I,6[=6].9    Tassa kammavipākena[^005-14] musāvādassa cūbhayaṃ puttamaṃsāni khādāmi pubbalohitamakkhitā[^005-15] ti.  || 34 ||  
Pañcaputtakhādakapetavatthu[^005-16] chattaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 006 -->
<a id="page-006"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 006]*

7 Sattaputtakhādakapetavatthu Vv_I,7[=7].1    Naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi duggandhā pūti vāyasi makkhikāhi[^006-1] parikiṇṇā[^006-1] kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasī ti.  || 35 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].2    Ahaṃ bhadante[^006-2] petī 'mhi duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ karitvāna  petalokaṃ ito gatā.  || 36 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].3    Kālena satta puttāni sāyaṃ satta punāpare vijāyitvāna khādāmi te pi nā[^006-3] honti me alaṃ.  || 37 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].4    Pariḍayhati dhūmāyati khudāya[^006-4] hadayaṃ mama nibbutiṃ nādhigacchāmi aggidaḍḍheva[^006-5] ātape ti.  || 38 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].5    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ[^006-6] kataṃ Kissa kammavipākena puttamaṃsāni khādasī ti.  || 39 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].6    Ahū mayhaṃ duve puttā ubho sampattayobbanā[^006-7] sāhaṃ puttabalūpetā[^006-8] sāmikaṃ atimaññisaṃ.[^006-9]  || 40 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].7    Tato me sāmiko kuddho sapattiṃ[^006-10] aññam ānayi[^006-11] sā ca gabbhaṃ alabhittha, tassā pāpaṃ acetayiṃ.  || 41 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].8    Sāhaṃ paduṭṭhamanasā akariṃ gabbhapātanaṃ tassā temāsiko gabbho pūtilohitako[^006-12] pati.  || 42 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].9    Tad'; assā mātā kupitā mayhaṃ ñātī samānayi sapathañ ca maṃ kāresi paribhāsāpayī[^006-13] ca maṃ.  || 43 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].10   [^006-14]Sāham ghorañ ca sapathaṃ musāvādaṃ abhāsisaṃ:[^006-15] puttamaṃsāni khādāmi sace taṃ pakataṃ mayā.  || 44 ||  
Vv_I,7[=7].11   Tassa kammavipākena[^006-16] musāvādassa cūbhayaṃ puttamaṃsāni khādāmi pubbalohitamakkhitā[^006-17] ti.  || 45 ||  
Sattaputtakhādakapetavatthu sattamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 007 -->
<a id="page-007"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 007]*

8 Goṇapetavatthu Vv_I,8[=8].1    Kin nu ummattarūpo va lāyitvā haritaṃ tiṇaṃ, khāda khādā ti lapasi gatasattaṃ jaraggavaṃ.  || 46 ||  
Vv_I,8[=8].2    Na hi annena pānena mato goṇo samuṭṭhahe tvaṃ si[^007-1] bālo ca dummedho yathā t'; añño va[^007-2] dummatī ti.  || 47 ||  
Vv_I,8[=8].3    Ime pādā idaṃ[^007-3] sīsaṃ ayaṃ kāyo savāladhi nettā tath'; eva tiṭṭhanti ayaṃ goṇo samuṭṭhahe.  || 48 ||  
Vv_I,8[=8].4    Nāyyakassa[^007-4] hatthapādā kāyo sīsañ ca dissati rudaṃ mattikathūpasmiṃ nanu tvañ ñeva[^007-5] dummatī[^007-5] ti.  || 49 ||  
Vv_I,8[=8].5    Ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ ghatasittaṃ va[^007-6] pāvakaṃ vārinā viya osiñcaṃ[^007-7] sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ.  || 50 ||  
Vv_I,8[=8].6    Abbūḷhaṃ[^007-8] vata me sallaṃ sokaṃ hadayanissitaṃ yo me sokaparetassa pitusokaṃ apānudi.  || 51 ||  
Vv_I,8[=8].7    Svāhaṃ abbūḷhasallo 'smi[^007-9] sītibhūto 'smi nibbuto na socāmi na rodāmi tava[^007-10] sutvāna māṇava.  || 52 ||  
Vv_I,8[=8].8    Evaṃ karonti sappaññā ye honti anukampakā vinivattayanti[^007-11] sokamhā Sujāto pitaraṃ yathā ti.  || 53 ||  
Goṇapetavatthu aṭṭhamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 008 -->
<a id="page-008"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 008]*

9 Mahāpesakārapetavatthu Vv_I,9[=9].1        [^008-1]Gūthañ ca muttaṃ ruhirañ[^008-2] ca pubbaṃ paribhuñjati kissa ayaṃ vipāko ayan nu[^008-3] kim kammam akāsi nārī yā[^008-4] sabbadā lohitapubbabhakkhā.  || 54 ||  
Vv_I,9[=9].2        Navāni vatthāni subhāni c'; eva mudūni suddhāni ca lomasāni dinnān'; imissā kiṭakā[^008-5] bhavanti ayaṃ nu kiṃ kammam akāsi nārī ti.  || 55 ||  
Vv_I,9[=9].3        Bhariyā mam'; esā ahū[^008-6] bhadante[^008-7] adāyikā maccharinī kadariyā sā maṃ dadantaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ akkosati[^008-8] paribhāsatī[^008-9] ca:  || 56 ||  
Vv_I,9[=9].4        Gūthañ ca muttaṃ ruhirañ[^008-2] ca pubbaṃ paribhuñja tvaṃ asuciṃ sabbakālaṃ etaṃ[^008-10] te paralokasmiṃ hotu vatthā[^008-11] ca te kiṭakasamā[^008-12] bhavantu[^008-13] etādisaṃ duccaritaṃ caritvā idhāgatā cirarattāya[^008-14] khādatī ti.  || 57 ||  
Mahāpesakārapetavatthu navamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 009 -->
<a id="page-009"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 009]*

10 Khallāṭiyapetavatthu Vv_I,10[=10].1    Kā nu antovimānasmiṃ tiṭṭhantī n'; upanikkhami[^009-1] upanikkhamassu bhadde tvaṃ[^009-2] passāma taṃ mahiddhikan[^009-3] ti.  || 58 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].2    Aṭṭiyāmi harāyāmi naggā nikkhamituṃ bahi keseh'; amhi paṭicchannā puññaṃ me appakaṃ katan ti.  || 59 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].3    Hand'; uttarīyaṃ[^009-4] dadāmi[^009-5] te imaṃ[^009-6] dussaṃ nivāsaya[^009-7] imaṃ[^009-6] dussaṃ nivasetvā bahi[^009-8] nikkhama sobhane, upanikkhamassu bhadde tvaṃ[^009-2] passāma tam bahiṭṭhitan[^009-9] ti.  || 60 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].4    Hatthena hatthe[^009-10] te dinnaṃ na mayhaṃ upakappati es'; etth'; upāsako saddho sammāsambuddhasāvako,  || 61 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].5    Etaṃ acchādayitvāna mama dakkhiṇam ādisa[^009-11] tathāhaṃ[^009-12] sukhitā hessaṃ sabbakāmasamiddhinī ti.  || 62 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].6    Tañ ca te nhāpayitvāna[^009-13] vilimpetvāna[^009-14] vāṇijā vattheh'; acchādayitvāna tassā dakkhiṇam ādisuṃ.  || 63 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].7   Samanantarānuddiṭṭhe[^009-15] vipāko upapajjatha[^009-16] bhojanacchādanapānīyaṃ,[^009-17] dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ.  || 64 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].8    Tato suddhā sucivasanā kāsikuttamadhārinī hasantī vimānā nikkhami dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalan ti.  || 65 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].9    Sucittarūpaṃ ruciraṃ vimānaṃ te pabhāsati[^009-18] devate pucchitācikkha kissa kammass'; idaṃ phalan ti.  || 66 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].10   Bhikkhuno caramānassa doṇinimmajjanaṃ[^009-19] ahaṃ adāsiṃ ujubhūtassa vippasannena cetasā.  || 67 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].11   Tassa kammassa kusalassa vipākaṃ dīgham antaraṃ anubhomi vimānasmiṃ tañ ca[^009-20] dāni[^009-20] parittakaṃ.  ||  
68 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 010 -->
<a id="page-010"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 010]*

Vv_I,10[=10].12   Uddhaṃ catūhi[^010-21] māsehi kālakiriyā[^010-22] bhavissati ekantaṃ[^010-23] kaṭukaṃ ghoraṃ nirayaṃ[^010-24] papatiss' ahaṃ.[^010-24]  || 69 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].13   Catukkaṇṇaṃ[^010-25] catudvāraṃ vibhattaṃ bhāgaso mitaṃ ayopākārapariyantaṃ ayasā paṭikujjitaṃ.  || 70 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].14   Tassa ayomayā bhūmi jalitā tejasā yutā samantā yojanasataṃ pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā.  || 71 ||  
Vv_I,10[=10].15   Tatthāhaṃ dīgham addhānam dukkham vedissa[^010-26] vedanaṃ [^010-27]phalañ ca pāpakammassa tasmā socām'; ahaṃ[^010-28] bhusan[^010-28] ti.  || 72 ||  
Khallāṭiyapetavatthu[^010-29] dasamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 011 -->
<a id="page-011"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 011]*

11 Nāgapetavatthu Vv_I,11[=11].1        Purato va[^011-1] setena paleti hatthinā majjhe pana assatarīrathena pacchā ca[^011-2] kaññā sivikāya[^011-3] niyyati[^011-3] obhāsayantī dasa sabbaso[^011-4] disā.  || 73 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].2        Tumhe pana[^011-5] muggarahatthapāṇino rudammukhā bhinnapabhinnagattā[^011-6] manussabhūtā kim akattha pāpaṃ yen'; aññamaññassa pivātha lohitan ti.  || 74 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].3        Purato va yo gacchati kuñjarena setena nāgena catukkamena amhāka[^011-7] putto ahu jeṭṭhako[^011-8] so[^011-8] dānāni datvāna sukhī[^011-9] pamodati.  || 75 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].4        Yo so majjhe assatarīrathena catubbhi yuttena suvaggitena amhāka[^011-7] putto ahu majjhimo so amaccharī dānapatī[^011-10] virocati.  || 76 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].5        Yā sā ca[^011-11] pacchā sivikāya niyyati[^011-12] nārī[^011-13] sapaññā migamandalocanā amhāka[^011-7] dhītā ahu sā kaniṭṭhā[^011-14] bhāgaḍḍhabhāgena sukhī pamodati.  || 77 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].6        Ete ca dānāni adaṃsu pubbe pasannacittā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ mayaṃ pana maccharino ahumhā[^011-15] paribhāsakā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ete padatvā[^011-16] paricārayanti mayañ ca sussāma naḷo va chinno[^011-17] ti.  || 78 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].7        Kiṃ tumhākaṃ bhojanaṃ kiṃ[^011-18] sayānaṃ[^011-18] kathaṃ su[^011-19] yāpetha supāpadhammino pahūtabhogesu anappakesu sukhaṃ virādhāya[^011-20] dukh'; ajja[^011-21] pattā ti.  || 79 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].8    Aññamaññaṃ vadhitvāna pivāma pubbalohitaṃ bahum pītvā[^011-22] na dhātā[^011-23] homa na cchādimhase[^011-24] mayaṃ.  || 80 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].9        Icc eva maccā[^011-25] paridevayanti adāyakā[^011-26] pecca[^011-27] Yamassa ṭhāyino ye te viditvā[^011-28] adhigamma bhoge na bhuñjare nā pi karonti puññaṃ.  ||  
81 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 012 -->
<a id="page-012"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 012]*

Vv_I,11[=11].10       Te khuppipāsūpagatā[^012-29] parattha petā[^012-30] ciraṃ jhāyare[^012-31] ḍayhamānā kammāni katvāna dukhudrayāni[^012-32] anubhonti dukkhaṃ kaṭukapphalāni.[^012-33]  || 82 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].11   Ittaraṃ[^012-34] hi dhanaṃ[^012-35] dhaññaṃ ittaraṃ[^012-34] idha jīvitaṃ ittaraṃ[^012-34] ittarato[^012-36] ñatvā dīpaṃ kayirātha[^012-37] paṇḍito.  || 83 ||  
Vv_I,11[=11].12   Ye te evaṃ pajānanti narā dhammassa kovidā te dāne na ppamajjanti sutvā arahataṃ vaco ti.  || 84 ||  
Nāgapetavatthu ekādasamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 013 -->
<a id="page-013"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 013]*

12 Uragapetavatthu Vv_I,12[=12].1    Urago va tacaṃ jiṇṇaṃ hitvā gacchati saṃ tanuṃ evaṃ sarīre nibbhoge pete kālakate[^013-1] sati,  || 85 ||  
Vv_I,12[=12].2    Ḍayhamāno na jānāti ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ tasmā etaṃ[^013-2] na rodāmi[^013-3] gato[^013-4] so tassa yā gatī ti.  || 86 ||  
Vv_I,12[=12].3    Anabbhito[^013-5] tato āgā[^013-6] nānuññāto[^013-7] ito gato yathāgato tathā gato tattha kā[^013-8] paridevanā.  || 87 ||  
Vv_I,12[=12].4    Ḍayhamāno na jānāti ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ tasmā etaṃ[^013-2] na rodāmi[^013-3] gato[^013-4] so tassa yā gatī ti.  || 88 ||  
Vv_I,12[=12].5    Sace rode kisā assaṃ tattha me kiṃ phalaṃ siyā ñātimittasuhajjānaṃ bhiyyo no aratī[^013-9] siyā.  || 89 ||  
Vv_I,12[=12].6    Ḍayhamāno na jānāti ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ[^013-10] tasmā etaṃ na rodāmi gato so tassa yā gatī ti.  || 90 ||  
Vv_I,12[=12].7    Yathā pi dārako candaṃ gacchantam anurodati evaṃ sampadam ev'; etaṃ yo petam anusocati.  || 91 ||  
Vv_I,12[=12].8    Ḍayhamāno na jānāti ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ tasmā etaṃ na rodāmi gato so tassa yā gatī ti.  || 92 ||  
Vv_I,12[=12].9    Yathā pi brahme udakumbho bhinno appaṭisandhiyo evaṃ sampadam ev'; etaṃ yo petam anusocati.  || 93 ||  
Vv_I,12[=12].10   Ḍayhamāno na jānāti ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ tasmā etaṃ na rodāmi gato so tassa yā gatī ti.  || 94 ||  
Uragapetavatthu dvādasamaṃ Tass'; uddānaṃ[^013-11]: [^013-12]Khettañ ca sūkaraṃ pūti piṭṭhañ cā pi tirokuḍḍaṃ[^013-13]

##### *[PTS Page 015]*

II UBBARIVAGGA 13 Saṃsāramocakapetavatthu Vv_II,1[=13].1    Naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi kisā[^015-1] dhamanisanthatā[^015-2] upphāsulike[^015-3] kisike[^015-4] kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasī ti.  || 95 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].2    Ahaṃ bhadante[^015-5] petī 'mhi duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokam ito gatā.  || 96 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].3    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ kissa kammavipākena petalokam ito gatā ti.  || 97 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].4        [^015-6]Anukampakā[^015-7] mayhaṃ nāhesuṃ bhante pitā ca[^015-8] mātā athavā pi ñātakā[^015-9] ye maṃ niyojeyyuṃ:[^015-10] dadāhi dānaṃ pasannacittā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ.  || 98 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].5        Ito ahaṃ vassasatāni pañca yaṃ evarūpā vicarāmi naggā khudāya[^015-11] taṇhāya ca[^015-12] khajjamānā pāpassa kammassa phalaṃ mamedaṃ.[^015-13]  || 99 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].6        Vandāmi taṃ ayya pasannacittā anukampa maṃ dhīra[^015-14] mahānubhāva[^015-15] datvā ca me ādissa[^015-16] yaṃ[^015-17] hi kiñci mocehi maṃ duggatiyā bhadante[^015-18] ti.[^015-19]  || 100 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].7    Sādhū ti so[^015-20] paṭissutvā[^015-21] Sāriputto 'nukampako[^015-22] bhikkhūnaṃ[^015-23] ālopaṃ datvā[^015-23] pāṇimattañ ca colakaṃ[^015-24] [^015-25]thālakassa ca pānīyaṃ tassā dakkhiṇam ādisi.  || 101 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].8    Samanantarānuddiṭṭhe[^015-26] vipāko upapajjatha[^015-27] bhojanacchādanapānīyaṃ,[^015-28] dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ.  ||  
102 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 016 -->
<a id="page-016"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 016]*

Vv_II,1[=13].9    Tato suddhā sucivasanā kāsikuttamadhārinī vicittavatthābharaṇā Sāriputtam upasaṅkamī ti.  || 103 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].10   Abhikkantena vaṇṇena yā tvaṃ tiṭṭhasi devate obhāsentī disā sabbā osadhī viya tārakā,  || 104 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].11  Kena t'; etādiso vaṇṇo kena te idha-m-ijjhati uppajjanti ca te bhogā ye keci manaso piyā.  || 105 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].12       Pucchāmi taṃ devi mahānubhāve manussabhūtā kim akāsi puññaṃ kenāsi evaṃ jalitānubhāvā vaṇṇo ca te sabbadisā pabhāsatī ti.  || 106 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].13   Uppaṇḍukiṃ[^016-29] kisaṃ chātaṃ naggaṃ[^016-30] āpatitacchaviṃ[^016-31] muni kāruṇiko loke taṃ maṃ[^016-32] addakkhi[^016-32] duggataṃ.  || 107 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].14   Bhikkhūnaṃ ālopaṃ datvā pāṇimattañ ca colakaṃ[^016-24] thālakassa ca pānīyaṃ mama dakkhiṇam ādisi.  || 108 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].15   Ālopassa phalaṃ passa bhattaṃ vassasataṃ dasa bhuñjāmi kāmakāminī anekarasavyañjanaṃ.  || 109 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].16   Pāṇimattassa colassa[^016-24] vipākaṃ passa yādisaṃ yāvatā Nandarājassa vijitasmiṃ paṭicchadā,  || 110 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].17   Tato bahutarā bhante vatthān'; acchādanāni[^016-33] me koseyyakambalīyāni[^016-34] khomakappāsikāni ca.  || 111 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].18   Vipulā ca mahagghā ca te p'; ākāse[^016-35] 'valambare sāhaṃ[^016-36] taṃ paridahāmi[^016-36] yaṃ yaṃ hi manaso piyaṃ.  || 112 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].19   Thālakassa ca pānīyaṃ vipākaṃ passa yādisaṃ gambhīrā[^016-37] caturassā ca pokkharañño[^016-38] sunimmitā.  || 113 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].20   Setodakā supatitthā[^016-39] sītā appaṭigandhiyā padumuppalasañchannā vārikiñjakkhapūritā.  || 114 ||  
Vv_II,1[=13].21   Sāhaṃ ramāmi kīḷāmi modāmi akutobhayā[^016-40] muniṃ kāruṇikaṃ loke[^016-41] bhante vanditum āgatā ti.  || 115 ||  
Saṃsāramocakapetavatthu paṭhamaṃ

Vv_II,2[=14].2    Ahaṃ te sakiyā mātā pubbe aññāsu[^018-4] jātisu[^018-5] upapannā[^018-6] pettivisayaṃ[^018-7] khuppipāsasamappitā.[^018-8]  || 117 ||  
Vv_II,2[=14].3    Chaḍḍitaṃ khipitaṃ kheḷaṃ[^018-9] siṅghāṇikaṃ silesumaṃ vasañ ca ḍayhamānānaṃ vijātānañ ca lohitaṃ,  || 118 ||

Vv_II,2[=14].8    Catasso kuṭiyo katvā saṅghe cātuddise[^018-21] adā kuṭiyo annapānañ ca mātu dakkhiṇam ādisi.[^018-22]  || 123 ||

Vv_II,3[=15].4    Caṇḍī ca pharusā c'; āsiṃ issukī[^020-6] maccharī saṭhī[^020-7] tāhaṃ durattuṃ vatvāna petalokam ito gatā ti.  || 137 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].5    Sabbaṃ[^020-8] aham pi jānāmi yathā tvaṃ caṇḍikā ahu aññañ ca kho taṃ[^020-9] pucchāmi kenāsi paṃsukuṇṭhitā.[^020-10]  || 138 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].6    Sīsaṃ[^020-11] nahātā[^020-11] tvaṃ[^020-12] āsi sucivatthā alaṅkatā ahañ ca kho taṃ[^020-13] adhimattaṃ[^020-14] samalaṅkatatarā tayā.  || 139 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].7    Tassā me pekkhamānāya sāmikena samantayi[^020-15] tato me issā vipulā kodho me samajāyatha.  || 140 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].8    Tato paṃsuṃ[^020-16] gahetvāna paṃsunā taṃ hi[^020-17] okiriṃ[^020-17] tassa kammavipākena ten'; amhi paṃsukuṇṭhitā.[^020-10]  || 141 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].9    Sabbaṃ[^020-8] aham pi jānāmi paṃsunā maṃ tvam okiri aññañ ca kho taṃ pucchāmi kena khajjasi[^020-18] kacchuyā.  || 142 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].10   Bhesajjahārī ubhayo vanantaṃ agamimhase tvañ ca bhesajjam āhari[^020-19] aham pi kapikacchuno.  || 143 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].11   Tassā tyājānamānaya[^020-20] seyyaṃ tyāhaṃ[^020-21] samokiriṃ[^020-22] tassa kammavipākena tena khajjāmi kacchuyā.  || 144 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].12   Sabbaṃ[^020-8] aham pi jānāmi seyyaṃ me tvaṃ samokiri aññañ ca kho taṃ pucchāmi kenāsi naggiyā tuvaṃ.  || 145 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].13   Sahāyānaṃ samayo āsi {ñātīnaṃ} samitī[^020-23] ahu tvañ ca āmantitā āsi sasāminī[^020-24] no ca kho ahaṃ.  || 146 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].14   Tassā tyājānamānāya[^020-20] dussaṃ tyāhaṃ apānudiṃ tassa kammavipākena ten'; amhi naggiyā ahaṃ.  || 147 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].15   Sabbaṃ[^020-8] aham pi jānāmi dussaṃ me tvaṃ apānudi aññañ ca kho taṃ pucchāmi kenāsi gūthagandhinī.[^020-25]  || 148 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].16   Tava gandhañ ca mālañ ca paccagghañ ca vilepanaṃ gūthakūpe atāresiṃ[^020-26] taṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ mayā. [^020-27]tassa kammavipākena ten'; amhi gūthagandhinī.  || 149 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].17   Sabbaṃ[^020-8] aham pi jānāmi taṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ tayā aññañ ca kho taṃ pucchāmi kenāsi duggatā tuvaṃ.  || 150 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].18   Ubhinnaṃ samakaṃ āsi yaṃ gehe vijjate dhanaṃ santesu deyyadhammesu dīpaṃ nākasim attano, tassa kammavipākena ten'; amhi duggatā ahaṃ.  ||  
151 ||

Vv_II,3[=15].19   Tad eva maṃ tvaṃ avaca pāpakammaṃ[^021-20] nisevasi na hi pāpehi kammehi sulabhāhosi[^021-29] suggati.[^021-30]  || 152 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].20   Vāmato maṃ tvaṃ paccesi atho pi maṃ usuyyasi[^021-31] passa pāpānaṃ kammānaṃ vipāko hoti yādiso.  || 153 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].21   Te[^021-32] gharadāsiyo āsuṃ[^021-32] tān'; evābharaṇān'; ime[^021-33] te c'; aññe[^021-34] paricārenti[^021-35] na bhogā honti sassatā.  || 154 ||

Vv_II,3[=15].28   Tato suddhā[^021-49] sucivasanā kāsikuttamadhārinī vicittavatthābharaṇā sapattiṃ[^021-50] upasaṅkami.  || 161 ||  
Vv_II,3[=15].29   Abhikkantena vaṇṇena yā tvaṃ tiṭṭhasi devate obhāsentī disā sabbā osadhī viya tārakā,  || 162 ||

Vv_II,3[=15].32   Ahaṃ Mattā tuvaṃ Tissā sapattī[^021-5] te pure ahuṃ pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokam ito gatā.  || 165 ||

16 Nandāpetavatthu Vv_II,4[=16].1    Kāḷī[^023-1] dubbaṇṇarūpāsi pharusā bhīrudassanā[^023-2] piṅgalāsi kaḷārāsi[^023-3] na taṃ maññāmi mānusin ti.  || 168 ||

169 ||. Vv_II,4[=16].3    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ kissa kammavipākena petalokam ito gatā ti.  || 170 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].4    Caṇḍī[^023-5] pharusavācā ca tayi c'; āsiṃ agāravā[^023-5] tāhaṃ duruttaṃ vatvāna petalokam ito gatā ti.  || 171 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].5    Hand'; uttarīyaṃ dadāmi te idaṃ[^023-6] dussaṃ nivāsaya imaṃ dussaṃ nivāsetvā ehi nessāmi[^023-7] taṃ gharaṃ.  || 172 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].6    Vatthañ ca annapānañ ca lacchasi tvaṃ gharaṃ gatā putte ca te passissasi suṇisāyo[^023-8] ca dakkhasi.  || 173 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].7    Hatthena hatthe te dinnaṃ na mayhaṃ upakappati bhikkhū ca sīlasampanne vītarāge bahussute,  || 174 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].8    Tappehi annapānena mama[^023-9] dakkhiṇam ādisa[^023-10] tadāhaṃ sukhitā hessaṃ sabbakāmasamiddhinī.  || 175 ||

##### *[PTS Page 024]*

Vv_II,4[=16].11   Samanantarānuddiṭṭhe vipāko upapajjatha[^024-17] bhojanacchādanapānīyaṃ[^024-18] dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ.  || 178 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].12   Tato suddhā sucivasanā kāsikuttamadhārinī vicittavatthābharaṇā sāmikaṃ upasaṅkami.  || 179 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].13  Abhikkantena vaṇṇena yā tvaṃ tiṭṭhasi devate obhāsentī disā sabbā osadhī viya tārakā,  || 180 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].14   Kena t'; etādiso vaṇṇo kena te idha-m-ijjhati uppajjanti ca te bhogā ye keci manaso piyā.  || 181 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].15       Pucchāmi taṃ devi mahānubhāve manussabhūtā kim akāsi puññaṃ kenāsi evaṃ jalitānubhāvā vaṇṇo ca te sabbadisā pabhāsatī ti.  || 182 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].16   Ahaṃ Nandā Nandasena[^024-4] bhariyā te pure ahuṃ pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokam ito gatā.  || 183 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].17   Tava dinnena dānena modāmi akutobhayā ciraṃ jīva gahapati saha sabbehi ñātibhi,[^024-19] asokaṃ virajaṃ ṭhānaṃ[^024-20] āvāsaṃ vasavattinaṃ.[^024-21]  || 184 ||  
Vv_II,4[=16].18   Idha dhammaṃ caritvāna dānaṃ datvāna gahapati vineyya maccheramalaṃ samūlaṃ anindito saggam upehi[^024-22] ṭhānan ti.  || 185 ||  
Nandāpetavatthu tatiyaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 025 -->
<a id="page-025"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 025]*

17 Maṭṭakuṇḍalīpetavatthu Vv_II,5[=17].1-21 See Vimānavatthu No. [^025-83].  ||  
186-206 ||  
Maṭṭakuṇḍalīpetavatthu pañcamaṃ 18 Kaṇhapetavatthu Vv_II,6[=18].1   Uṭṭhehi Kaṇha[^025-1] kiṃ[^025-2] sesi ko attho supanena[^025-3] te yo ca tuyhaṃ sako bhātā hadayaṃ cakkhuṃ[^025-4] va[^025-4] dakkhiṇaṃ, tassa vātā balīyanti Ghato[^025-5] jappati Kesavā ti.  || 207 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].2    Tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā Rohiṇeyyassa Kesavo taramānarūpo vuṭṭhāsi[^025-6] bhātusokena aṭṭito.[^025-7]  || 208 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].3    Kin nu ummattarūpo va kevalaṃ Dvārakaṃ imaṃ saso saso ti lapasi kīdisaṃ[^025-8] sasam icchasi.[^025-8]  || 209 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].4    Sovaṇṇamayaṃ maṇimayaṃ lohamayaṃ atha rūpiyāmayaṃ[^025-9] saṅkhasilāpavāḷamayaṃ kārayissāmi te sasaṃ.  || 210 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].5    Santi aññe pi sasakā araññe[^025-10] vanagocarā te pi te ānayissāmi kīdisaṃ sasam icchasī ti.  || 211 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].6    Nāham[^025-11] ete sase icche[^025-11] ye sasā paṭhavissitā[^025-12] candato sasam icchāmi taṃ me ohara Kesavā ti.  || 212 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].7    So nūna[^025-13] madhuraṃ ñāti jīvitaṃ vijahissasi[^025-14] apatthiyaṃ[^025-15] patthayasi candato sasam icchasī[^025-16] ti.  || 213 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].8    Evañ ce[^025-17] Kaṇha jānāsi yath'; aññam anusāsasi kasmā pure mataṃ puttaṃ ajjāpi[^025-18]-m-anusocasī ti.  || 214 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].9    Ye[^025-19] na[^025-19] labbhā manussena amanussena vā pana:[^025-20] jāto me mā marī[^025-21] putto kuto labbhā alabbhiyaṃ.  || 215 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].10   Na mantā mūlabhesajjā osadhehi[^025-22] dhanena vā sakkā ānayituṃ Kaṇha yaṃ petam anusocasi.  ||  
216 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 026 -->
<a id="page-026"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 026]*

Vv_II,6[=18].11   [^026-23]Mahaddhanā mahābhogā raṭṭhavanto pi khattiyā pahūtadhanadhaññāse[^026-24] te pi no ajarāmarā.  || 217 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].12   Khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā suddā caṇḍālapukkusā ete c'; aññe[^026-25] ca jātiyā te pi no ajarāmarā.  || 218 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].13   Ye mantaṃ parivattenti[^026-26] chaḷaṅgaṃ brahmacintitaṃ ete c'; aññe[^026-25] ca vijjāya te pi no ajarāmarā.  || 219 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].14   Isayo vā pi ye santā saññatattā tapassino sarīram te pi kālena vijahanti tapassino.  || 220 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].15   Bhāvitattā arahanto[^026-27] katakiccā anāsavā nikkhipanti imaṃ dehaṃ puññapāpaparikkhayā ti.  || 221 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].16   Ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ ghatasittaṃ va pāvakaṃ vārinā viya osiñcaṃ[^026-28] sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ.  || 222 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].17   Abbūḷhaṃ[^026-29] vata me sallaṃ sokaṃ badayanissitaṃ[^026-30] yo me sokaparetassa puttasokaṃ apānudi.  || 223 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].18   So 'haṃ[^026-31] abhuḷhasallo 'smi sītibhūto 'smi nibbuto na socāmi na rodāmi tava sutvāna bhāsitaṃ.[^026-32]  || 224 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].19   Evaṃ karonti sappaññā ye honti anukampakā vinivattayanti[^026-33] sokamhā Ghato[^026-5] jeṭṭhaṃ va bhātaraṃ.  || 225 ||  
Vv_II,6[=18].20   Yassa etādisā honti amaccā[^026-34] paricārakā[^026-35] subhāsitena anvesi[^026-36] Ghato[^026-5] jeṭṭhaṃ va bhātaran ti.  || 226 ||  
Kaṇhapetavatthu chaṭṭhaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 027 -->
<a id="page-027"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 027]*

19 Dhanapālapetavatthu Vv_II,7[=19].1    Naggo dubbaṇṇarūpo 'si kiso dhamanisanthato[^027-1] upphāsuliko kisiko ko nu tvaṃ asi mārisā ti.  || 227 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].2    Ahaṃ bhadante[^027-2] peto 'mhi duggato Yamalokiko pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokam ito gato ti.  || 228 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].3    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ kissa kammavipākena petalokam ito gato ti.  || 229 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].4    Nagaraṃ atthi Dasaṇṇānam[^027-3] Erakacchan ti vissutaṃ tattha seṭṭhi pure āsiṃ Dhanapālo ti maṃ vidū.[^027-4]  || 230 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].5    Asīti sakaṭavāhānaṃ[^027-5] hiraññassa ahosi me pahūtaṃ me jātarūpaṃ muttā vaḷuriyā bahū.  || 231 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].6    Tāva mahaddhanassāpi[^027-6] na me dātuṃ piyaṃ ahu pidahitvā dvāraṃ bhuñjāmi:[^027-7] mā maṃ yācanakāddasuṃ.[^027-8]  || 232 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].7    Assaddho maccharī c'; āsiṃ[^027-9] kadariyo paribhāsako dadantānaṃ karontānaṃ vārayissaṃ[^027-10] bahujjanaṃ:[^027-11]  ||  
233 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 028 -->
<a id="page-028"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 028]*

Vv_II,7[=19].8    Vipāko natthi dānassa saññamassa kuto phalaṃ pokkharaññodapānāni ārāmāni ca ropite papāyo ca vināsesiṃ dugge saṅkamanāni ca.  || 234 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].9    Svāhaṃ akatakalyāṇo katapāpo tato cuto upapanno[^028-12] pettivisayaṃ[^028-13] khuppipāsasamapito.[^028-14]  || 235 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].10   [^028-15]Pañcapaṇṇāsavassāni yato[^028-16] kālakato[^028-17] ahaṃ nābhijānāmi bhuttaṃ vā pītaṃ vā pana pāniyaṃ.[^028-18]  || 236 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].11   Yo saṃyamo so vināso yo vināso so saṃyamo petā hi kira jānanti yo[^028-19] saṃyamo so vināso.  || 237 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].12   [^028-20]Ahaṃ pure saṃyamissaṃ nādāsiṃ bahuke dhane santesu deyyadhammesu dīpaṃ nakāsim attano.  || 238 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].13   Svāhaṃ[^028-21] pacchānutappāmi attakammaphalūpago[^028-22] uddhaṃ catūhi[^028-23] māsehi kālakiriyā[^028-24] bhavissati, ekantaṃ[^028-25] kaṭukaṃ ghoraṃ nirayaṃ papatiss'; ahaṃ.  || 239 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].14   Catukkaṇṇaṃ[^028-26] catudvāraṃ vibhattaṃ bhāgaso mitaṃ ayopākārapariyantaṃ ayasā paṭikujjitaṃ.  || 240 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].15   Tassa ayomayā bhūmi jalitā tejasā yutā[^028-27] samantā yojanasataṃ pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā.  || 241 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].16   Tatthāhaṃ dīgham addhānaṃ dukkhaṃ vedissa vedanaṃ phalaṃ pāpassa kammassa tasmā socām'; ahaṃ[^028-28] bhusaṃ.[^028-28]  || 242 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].17   Taṃ vo vadāmi bhaddaṃ vo[^028-29] yāvant'; ettha samāgatā mākattha pāpakaṃ kammaṃ āvī[^028-30] vā yadi vā raho.  || 243 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].18   Sace taṃ pāpakaṃ kammaṃ karissatha karotha vā na vo[^028-29] dukkhā pamutt'; atthi[^028-31] uppaccāpi[^028-32] palāyataṃ.[^028-33]  || 244 ||  
Vv_II,7[=19].19   Matteyyā hotha petteyyā kule jeṭṭhāpacāyikā[^028-34] sāmaññā hotha brahmaññā evaṃ saggaṃ gamissathā ti.[^028-35]  || 245 ||  
Dhanapālapetavatthu sattamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 029 -->
<a id="page-029"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 029]*

20 Cūḷaseṭṭhipetavatthu[^029-1] Vv_II,8[=20].1        Naggo kiso pabbajito 'si bhante rattiṃ kuhiṃ gacchasi kissa hetu ācikkha me taṃ api sakkuṇemu sabbena vittaṃ paṭipādaye tuvan ti.  || 246 ||  
Vv_II,8[=20].2        Bārāṇasī[^029-2] nagaraṃ dūraghuṭṭhaṃ tatthāhaṃ gahapati aḍḍhako dīno[^029-3] adātā gedhitamano[^029-4] āmisasmiṃ dussīlyena[^029-5] Yamavisayamhi patto.  || 247 ||  
Vv_II,8[=20].3        So sūcikāya kilamito[^029-6] tehi ten'; eva ñātisu[^029-7] yāmi āmisakiñcihetu[^029-8] adānasīlā na ca saddahanti: dānaphalaṃ[^029-9] hoti paramhi loke.  ||  
248 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 030 -->
<a id="page-030"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 030]*

Vv_II,8[=20].4        Dhītā[^030-10] ca mayhaṃ lapate[^030-11] abhikkhaṇaṃ; dassāmi dānaṃ pitunnaṃ[^030-12] pitāmahānaṃ upakkhaṭaṃ[^030-13] parivisayanti[^030-14] brāhmaṇe[^030-15] yāmy'; ahaṃ[^030-16] Andhakavindaṃ[^030-17] bhottuṃ.[^030-18]  || 249 ||  
Vv_II,8[=20].5        Tam avoca rājā: anubhaviyāna[^030-19] tam pi[^030-20] eyyāsi khippaṃ aham pi karissaṃ[^030-21] pūjaṃ ācikkha me taṃ yadi atthi hetu saddhāyitaṃ hetuvaco[^030-22] suṇomi.[^030-23]  || 250 ||  
Vv_II,8[=20].6        Tathā ti vatvā agamāsi tattha bhuñjiṃsu bhattaṃ na[^030-24] ca pana[^030-24] dakkhiṇārahā[^030-25] paccāgami[^030-26] Rājagahaṃ punāparaṃ pātur ahosi purato janādhipassa.  || 251 ||  
Vv_II,8[=20].7        Disvāna petaṃ. punar eva[^030-27] āgataṃ rājā avoca: aham api[^030-28] kiṃ dadāmi ācikkha me taṃ yadi atthi hetu yena tuvaṃ[^030-29] cirataraṃ pīṇito siyā.  || 252 ||  
Vv_II,8[=20].8        Buddhañ ca saṅghaṃ parivisiyāna rāja annena pānena pi[^030-30] cīvarena taṃ dakkhiṇaṃ ādisā me hitāya evaṃ ahaṃ cirataraṃ pīṇito siyā.  || 253 ||  
Vv_II,8[=20].9        Tato ca rājā nipatitva[^030-31] tāvade[^030-32] dānaṃ sahatthā atulaṃ daditvā[^030-33] saṅghe[^030-34] ārocayī[^030-35] pakatiṃ[^030-36] tathāgatassa tassa[^030-37] ca[^030-37] petassa dakkhiṇaṃ[^030-38] ādisittha.  || 254 ||  
Vv_II,8[=20].10       So pūjito ativiya sobhamāno pātur ahosi purato janādhipassa: yakkho 'ham asmi[^030-39] paramiddhipatto na mayham iddhi[^030-40] samasadisā[^030-41] mānusā.[^030-42]  || 255 ||  
Vv_II,8[=20].11       Passānubhāvaṃ aparimitaṃ mamedaṃ tayānuddiṭṭhaṃ[^030-43] atulaṃ daditvā[^030-44] saṅghe santappito satataṃ sadā bahūhi yāmi[^030-45] ahaṃ[^030-45] sukhito manussadevā ti.  || 256 ||  
Cūḷaseṭṭhipetavatthu[^030-1] aṭṭhamaṃ BHĀṆAVĀRAṂ PAṬHAMAṂ

21 Aṅkurapetavatthu Vv_II,9[=21].1    Yassa atthāya gacchāma Kambojaṃ dhanahārakā ayaṃ kāmadado yakkho imaṃ yakkhaṃ nīyāmase.[^032-1]  || 257 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].2    Imaṃ yakkhaṃ gahetvāna sādhukena pasayha vā yānaṃ āropayitvāna khippaṃ gacchāma Dvāraka ti.  || 258 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].3    Yassa rukkhassa chāyaya nisīdeyya sayeyya vā na tassa sākhaṃ bhindeyya[^032-2] mittadubbho hi pāpako ti.  || 259 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].4    Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya nisīdeyya sayeyya vā khandham[^032-3] pi tassa chindeyya attho ce tādiso siyā ti.  || 260 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].5    Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya nisīdeyya sayeyya vā na tassa pattaṃ bhindeyya[^032-4] mittadubbho hi pāpako ti.  || 261 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].6    Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya nisīdeyya sayeyya vā samūlam[^032-5] pi taṃ abhuyha[^032-6] attho ce[^032-7] tādiso[^032-7] siyā ti.  || 262 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].7        Yass'; ekarattim pi[^032-8] ghare vaseyya Yatth'[^032-9] annapānaṃ puriso labhetha na tassa pāpaṃ manasā pi cetaye[^032-10] kataññutā sappurisehi vaṇṇitā.  || 263 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].8        Yass'; ekarattim pi ghare vaseyya annena[^032-11] pānena upaṭṭhito siyā na tassa pāpaṃ manasā pi cetaye[^032-10] adubbhapāṇī[^032-12] dahate mittadubbhiṃ.[^032-13]  || 264 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].9    Yo pubbe katakalyāṇo pacchā pāpena hiṃsati allapāṇihato[^032-14] poso na so bhadrāni passatī ti.[^032-15]  || 265 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].10       Nāhaṃ devena vā[^032-16] manussena vā issariyena vāhaṃ suppasayho[^032-17] yakkho 'ham asmi paramiddhipatto dūraṅgamo vaṇṇabalūpapanno ti.  || 266 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].11   Pāṇi te sabbasovaṇṇo pañcadhāro madhussavo nānā rasā paggharanti maññe 'haṃ taṃ purindadaṃ.  || 267 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].12   N'; amhi[^032-18] devo na gandhabbo nāpi Sakko purindado petaṃ maṃ[^032-19] Aṅkura jānāhi Bheruvamhā[^032-20] idhāgatan ti.  ||  
268 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 033 -->
<a id="page-033"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 033]*

Vv_II,9[=21].13   Kiṃsīlo kiṃsamācāro Bheruvasmiṃ[^033-20] pure tuvaṃ kena te brahmacariyena puññaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhati.  || 269 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].14   Tunnavāyo[^033-21] pure āsiṃ Bheruvasmiṃ[^033-20] tadā ahaṃ sukicchavutti kapaṇo na me vijjati dātave.  || 270 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].15   Āvesanañ[^033-22] ca me āsi Asayhassa upantike saddhassa dānapatino katapuññassa lajjino.  || 271 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].16   Tattha yācanakā yanti {nānāgottā} vanibbakā te ca maṃ tattha pucchanti Asayhassa nivesanaṃ:  || 272 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].17   Kattha[^033-23] gacchāma[^033-23] bhaddaṃ vo kattha dānaṃ padīyati.[^033-24] Tesāhaṃ[^033-25] puṭṭho vakkhāmi Asayhassa nivesanaṃ,  || 273 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].18   Paggayha dakkhiṇaṃ bāhuṃ: ettha gacchatha bhaddaṃ vo ettha dānaṃ padīyati[^033-24] Asayhassa nivesane.[^033-26]  || 274 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].19   Tena pāṇi kāmadado tena pāṇi madhussavo tena me brahmacariyena puññaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhatī ti.  || 275 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].20   Na kira tvaṃ adā dānaṃ sakapāṇīhi kassaci parassa dānaṃ anumodamāno pāṇiṃ paggayha pāvadi.  || 276 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].21   Tena pāni kāmadado tena pāṇi madhussavo tena te[^033-27] brahmacariyena puññaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhati.  || 277 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].22   Yo so dānam adā bhante pasanno sakapāṇihi[^033-28] so hitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ kin nu so disetaṃ gato.  || 278 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].23       Nāhaṃ pajānāmi[^033-29] asayhasāhino[^033-30] aṅgīrasassa gatim āgatiṃ vā sutañ ca me Vessavaṇassa santike sakkassa sahavyataṃ gato Asayho.  || 279 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].24   Alam eva kātuṃ kalyāṇaṃ dānaṃ dātuṃ yathārahaṃ pāṇiṃ[^033-31] kāmadadaṃ disvā ko puññaṃ na karissati.  || 280 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].25   So hi nūna ito gantvā anuppatvāna Dvārakaṃ dānaṃ[^033-32] paṭṭhāpayissāmi[^033-33] yaṃ mam'; assa sukhāvahaṃ.  || 281 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].26   Dassām'; annañ ca pānañ ca[^033-34] vatthasenāsanāni ca papañ ca udapānañ ca dugge saṅkamanāni cā ti.  || 282 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].27   Kena te aṅgulī kuṇṭhā[^033-35] mukhañ ca kuṇḍalīkataṃ[^033-36] akkhīni ca paggharanti kiṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ tayā ti.  ||  
283 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 034 -->
<a id="page-034"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 034]*

Vv_II,9[=21].28   Aṅgīrarasassa gahapatino saddhassa gharam esino tassāhaṃ dānavissagge dāne[^034-37] adhikato ahuṃ.[^034-38]  || 284 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].29   Tattha yācanake disvā āgate bhojanatthike ekam antaṃ apakkamma akāsiṃ kuṇḍalīmukhaṃ.[^034-39]  || 285 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].30   Tena me aṅgulī kuṇṭhā[^034-35] mukhañ ca kuṇḍalīkataṃ[^034-36] akkhīni ca[^034-40] paggharanti taṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃmayā ti.  || 286 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].31   Dhammena te kāpurisa mukhañ ca kuṇḍalīkataṃ[^034-36] akkhīni ca paggharanti[^034-41] yaṃ tvaṃ[^034-42] parassa dānassa akāsi kuṇḍalīmukhan[^034-39] ti.  || 287 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].32   Kathaṃ hi dānaṃ dadamāno kareyya parapattiyaṃ annapānaṃ[^034-43] khādanīyaṃ vatthasenāsanāni ca ti.  || 288 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].33   So hi nūna ito gantvā anuppatvāna Dvārakaṃ dānaṃ paṭṭhāpayissāmi[^034-33] yaṃ mam'; assa sukhāvahaṃ.  || 289 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].34   Dassām'; annañ ca pānañ ca vatthasenāsanāni ca papañ ca udapānañ ca dugge[^034-44] saṅkamanāni ca.  || 290 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].35   Tato hi so nivattitvā anuppatvāna Dvārakaṃ dānaṃ paṭṭhayi[^034-45] Aṅkuro yaṃ tumassa[^034-46] sukhāvahaṃ.  || 291 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].36   Adā annañ ca pānañ ca vatthasenāsanāni ca papañ ca udapānañ ca vippasannena cetasā:  || 292 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].37   Ko chāto ko ca tasito ko vatthaṃ parivassati[^034-47] kassa santāni yoggāni ito yojentu vāhanaṃ.  || 293 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].38   Ko chatt'; icchati gandhañ ca ko mālaṃ ko upāhanaṃ iti ssu[^034-48] tattha ghosenti kappakā sūda[^034-49]-māgadhā[^034-50] sadā sayañ ca pāto ca Aṅkurassa nivesane ti.  || 294 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].39   Sukhaṃ supati Aṅkuro iti jānāti maṃ jano dukkhaṃ supāmi Sindhaka[^034-51] yaṃ na passāmi yācake.  || 295 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].40   Sukhaṃ supati Aṅkuro iti jānāti maṃ jano dukkhaṃ supāmi[^034-52] Sindhaka[^034-51] appake su vanibbake ti.  || 296 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].41   Sakko ce te varaṃ dajjā Tāvatiṃsānam issaro Kissa sabbassa lokassa varamāno varaṃ vare ti.  || 297 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].42   Sakko ce me varaṃ dajjā Tāvatiṃsānam issaro kāluṭṭhitassa me sato suriyass'; uggamanaṃ[^034-53] pati [^034-54]dibbā bhakkhā pātubhaveyyuṃ sīlavanto ca yācakā.  ||  
298 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 035 -->
<a id="page-035"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 035]*

Vv_II,9[=21].43.  Dadato me na khīyetha datvā nānutappeyy'; ahaṃ[^035-55] dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādeyyaṃ[^035-56] evaṃ[^035-57] Sakkaṃ[^035-58] varaṃ vare ti.  || 299 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].44       Na sabbavittāni pare pavecche dadeyya dānañ ca dhanañ ca rakkhe tasmā hi dānā dhanam eva seyyo atippadānena kulā na honti.  || 300 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].45       Adānaṃ atidānañ ca nappasaṃsanti[^035-59] paṇḍitā tasmā hi dānā dhanam eva seyyo samena vatteyya sa dhīradhammo ti.  || 301 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].46       Aho vatā[^035-60] re aham eva dajjaṃ santo hi[^035-61] maṃ sappurisā bhajeyyuṃ[^035-62] megho va ninnān'; abhipūrayanto[^035-63] santappaye sabbavanibbakānaṃ.  || 302 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].47   Yassa yācanake disvā mukhavaṇṇo pasīdati datvā attamano hoti taṃ gharaṃ vasato sukhaṃ.  || 303 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].48   Yassa yācanake disvā mukhavaṇṇo pasīdati datvā attamano hoti esā puññassa[^035-64] sampadā.  || 304 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].49   Pubbe va dānā sumano dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādaye[^035-65] datvā attamano hoti eso puññassa[^035-64] sampadā ti.  || 305 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].50   Saṭṭhivāhasahassāni Aṅkurassa nivesane bhojanaṃ dīyate niccaṃ puññapekkhassa jantuno.  || 306 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].51   Tisahassāni[^035-66] sūdā hi[^035-66] āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā Aṅkuraṃ upajūvanti dāne yaññassa vyāvaṭā.[^035-67]  || 307 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].52   Saṭṭhipurisasahassāni[^035-68] āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā Aṅkurassa mahādāne kaṭṭhaṃ phālenti māṇavā.  || 308 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].53   Soḷasitthisahassāni sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā Aṅkurassa mahādāne vidhā piṇḍenti nāriyo.  || 309 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].54   Soḷasitthisahassāni sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā Aṅkurassa mahādāne dabbigāhā upaṭṭhitā.[^035-69]  || 310 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].55   Bahuṃ bahunnaṃ[^035-70] pādāsi ciraṃ pādāsi khattiyo[^035-71] sakkaccañ ca sahatthā ca[^035-72] cittīkatvā[^035-73] punappunaṃ.  || 311 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].56   Bahū[^035-74] māse ca[^035-75] pakkhe ca utu-saṃvaccharāni ca mahādānaṃ pavattesi Aṅkuro dīgham antaraṃ.  ||  
312 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 036 -->
<a id="page-036"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 036]*

Vv_II,9[=21].57   Evaṃ datvā yajitvā ca Aṅkuro dīgham antaraṃ so[^036-76] hitvā mānusaṃ deham Tāvatiṃsūpago ahū ti.  || 313 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].58   Kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ datvāna Anuruddhassa Indako so hitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ Tāvatiṃsūpago ahū.  || 314 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].59   Dasahi ṭhānehi Aṅkuraṃ Indako atirocati rūpe sadde rase gandhe phoṭṭhabbe[^036-77] ca manorame,  || 315 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].60  Āyunā yasasā c'; eva vaṇṇena ca sukhena ca ādhipaccena Aṅkuraṃ Indako atirocatī ti.[^036-78]  || 316 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].61   Tāvatiṃse yadā buddho sīlāyaṃ paṇḍukambale pāricchattakamūlamhi[^036-79] vihāsi purisuttamo,  || 317 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].62   Dasasu lokadhātūsu sannipatitvāna devatā payirupāsanti sambuddhaṃ vasantaṃ nagamuddhani.  || 318 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].63   Na koci devo vaṇṇena sambuddhaṃ atirocati sabbe deve adhigayha[^036-80] sambuddho va virocati.  || 319 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].64   Yojanāni dasa dve[^036-81] ca[^036-81] Aṅkuro 'yaṃ tadā ahu avidūre sambuddhassa[^036-82] Indako atirocati.  || 320 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].65   Oloketvāna sambuddho Aṅkurañ cāpi Indakaṃ dakkhiṇeyyaṃ pabhāvento[^036-83] idaṃ vacanam abravi:[^036-84]  || 321 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].66   Mahādānaṃ tayā dinnaṃ Aṅkura dīgham antaraṃ atidūre[^036-85] nisinno 'si āgaccha mama santike.[^036-86]  || 322 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].67   Codito bhāvitattena Aṅkuro idam abravi:[^036-84] kiṃ mayhaṃ tena dānena dakkhiṇeyyena suññataṃ.  || 323 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].68   Ayaṃ so Indako yakkho dajjā dānaṃ parittakaṃ atirocati amhe hi cando tāragaṇe yathā.  || 324 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].69   Ujjaṅgale[^036-87] yathā khette bījaṃ bahukam pi[^036-88] ropitaṃ na vipulaṃ[^036-89] phalaṃ[^036-90] hoti na pi toseti kassakaṃ.  || 325 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].70   Tath'; eva dānaṃ bahukaṃ dussīlesu patiṭṭhitaṃ na vipulaṃ[^036-89] phalaṃ[^036-90] hoti na pi toseti dāyakaṃ.[^036-91]  || 326 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].71   Yathā pi bhaddake khette bījaṃ appam pi[^036-92] ropitaṃ[^036-92] sammādhāraṃ pavecchante phalaṃ toseti kassakaṃ,[^036-93]  || 327 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].72   Tath'; eva sīlavantesu guṇavantesu tādisu appakam pi kataṃ kāraṃ puññaṃ hoti mahaphalan ti.  ||  
328 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 037 -->

##### *[PTS Page 037]*

Vv_II,9[=21].73   Viceyya dānam dātabbaṃ yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ viceyya dānaṃ datvāna saggaṃ gacchanti dāyakā.  || 329 ||  
Vv_II,9[=21].74       Viceyya dānaṃ sugatappasatthaṃ[^037-94] ye dakkhiṇeyyā idha jīvaloke etesu dinnāni mahapphalāni bījāni vuttāni yathā sukhette ti.  || 330 ||

22 Uttaramātupetavatthu Vv_II,10[=22].1    Divāvihāragataṃ[^039-1] bhikkhuṃ Gaṅgātīre nisinnakaṃ taṃ petī upasaṅkamma dubbaṇṇā bhīrudassanā,  || 331 ||  
Vv_II,10[=22].2    Kesā c'; assā atidīghā[^039-2] yāva bhūmāvalambare[^039-3] Kesehi sā paṭicchannā samaṇaṃ etad abravī:[^039-4]  || 332 ||  
Vv_II,10[=22].3    Pañcapaṇṇāsavassāni yato kālakatā[^039-5] ahaṃ nābhijānāmi bhuttaṃ vā pītaṃ vā pana pāniyaṃ[^039-6] dehi tvaṃ[^039-7] pāniyaṃ[^039-8] bhante tasitā pāniyāya[^039-9] me ti.  || 333 ||  
Vv_II,10[=22].4    Ayaṃ sītodikā[^039-10] Gaṅgā Himavantato[^039-11] sandati Piva etto gahetvāna kiṃ maṃ yācasi pāniyaṃ.[^039-12]  || 334 ||  
Vv_II,10[=22].5    Sacāhaṃ bhante Gaṅgāya[^039-13] sayaṃ gaṇhāmi pāniyaṃ[^039-6] lohitaṃ me parivattati tasmā yācāmi pāniyaṃ.[^039-12]  || 335 ||  
Vv_II,10[=22].6    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ kissa kammavipākena[^039-14] Gaṅgā te hoti lohitaṃ.[^039-15]  || 336 ||  
Vv_II,10[=22].7    Putto[^039-16] me Uttaro nāma[^039-16] saddho āsi upāsako so ca mayhaṃ akāmāya samaṇānaṃ pavecchati cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañ ca paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ.[^039-17]  || 337 ||  
Vv_II,10[=22].8    Tam ahaṃ paribbhāsāmi maccherena upaddutā: yaṃ tvaṃ[^039-18] mayhaṃ akāmāya samaṇānaṃ pavecchasi[^039-19] cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañ ca paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ,  || 338 ||  
Vv_II,10[=22].9    Etaṃ te paralokasmiṃ lohitaṃ hotu Uttara tassa kammavipākena[^039-20] Gaṅgā me hoti lohitan ti.  || 339 ||  
Uttaramātupetavatthu dasamaṃ

23 Suttapetavatthu Vv_II,11[=23].1        Ahaṃ pure pabbajitassa bhikkhuno suttaṃ adāsiṃ[^040-1] upagamma[^040-2] yācitā[^040-3] tassa vipāko vipula[^040-4]-phalūpalabbhati bahū[^040-5] ca me uppajjare[^040-6] {vatthakotiyo.}  || 340 ||  
Vv_II,11[=23].2        Pupphābhikiṇṇaṃ ramitaṃ[^040-7] vimānaṃ anekacittaṃ naranārisevitaṃ[^040-8] sāhaṃ bhuñjāmi ca pārupāmi ca pahūtavittā[^040-9] na ca tāva khīyati.  || 341 ||  
Vv_II,11[=23].3        Tass'; eva kammassa vipākam anvayā sukhañ ca sātañ ca idhūpalabbhati sāhaṃ gantvā punad eva[^040-10] mānusaṃ kāhāmi puññāni nay'[^040-11] ayyaputta man ti.[^040-12]  || 342 ||  
Vv_II,11[=23].4        Satta tuvaṃ vassasatā idhāgatā jiṇṇā ca vuddhā[^040-13] ca tahiṃ bhavissasi sabbe va[^040-14] te kālakatā[^040-15] ca[^040-16] ñātakā Kiṃ[^040-17] tattha gantvāna ito karissasī ti.[^040-12]  || 343 ||  
Vv_II,11[=23].5        Satt'; eva vassāni idhāgatāya me dibbañ ca sukhañ ca samappitāya sāhaṃ gantvā[^040-18] punar[^040-19] eva mānusaṃ kāhāmi puññāni nay'[^040-11] ayyaputta man ti.[^040-12]  ||  
344 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 041 -->
<a id="page-041"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 041]*

Vv_II,11[=23].6        So taṃ gahetvāna pasayha bāhāyaṃ paccānayitvāna[^041-20] theriṃ sudubbalaṃ vajjesi[^041-21] aññam pi janaṃ idhāgataṃ: karotha puññāni sukhūpalabbhatī ti.[^041-12]  || 345 ||  
Vv_II,11[=23].7        Diṭṭhā mayā akatena sādhunā petā vihaññanti tath'; eva mānusā[^041-22] kammañ ca katvā sukhavedanīyaṃ devā manussā ca sukhe ṭhitā pajā ti.  || 346 ||  
&1 Suttapetavatthu ekādasamaṃ 24 Kaṇṇamuṇḍapetavatthu Vv_II,12[=24].1    Soṇṇasopānaphalakā[^041-1] soṇṇavālukasanthatā[^041-2] tattha sogandhiyā[^041-3] vaggu sucigandhā manoramā.  || 347 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].2    Nānārukkhehi sañchannā nānāgandhasameritā[^041-4] nānāpadumasañchannā puṇḍarīkasamotatā.[^041-5]  || 348 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].3    Surabhī[^041-6] sampavāyanti manuññā māluteritā haṃsakoñcābhirudā[^041-7] cakkavākābhikūjitā.[^041-8]  ||  
349 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 042 -->
<a id="page-042"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 042]*

Vv_II,12[=24].4    Nānādijagaṇākiṇṇā[^042-9] nānāsaragaṇāyutā[^042-10] nānāphaladharā[^042-11] rukkhā nānāpupphadharā[^042-12] vanā.  || 350 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].5    Na manussesu īdisaṃ nagaraṃ yādisaṃ idaṃ pāsādā[^042-13] bahukā tuyhaṃ sovaṇṇarūpiyāmayā,[^042-14] daddallamānā ābhenti[^042-15] samantā caturo disā.[^042-16]  || 351 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].6    Pañca dāsisatā[^042-17] tuyhaṃ yā te 'mā paricārikā tā[^042-18] kambukāyūradharā[^042-19] kañcanāveḷabhūsitā.[^042-20]  || 352 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].7    Pallaṅkā bahukā tuyhaṃ sovannarūpiyāmāyā[^042-21] kadalimigasañchannā[^042-22] sajjā[^042-234] goṇakasanthatā,[^042-24]  || 353 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].8    Yattha tvaṃ[^042-25] vāsūpagatā sabbakāmasamiddhinī.[^042-26] sampattāy'; aḍḍharattāya[^042-27] tato uṭṭhāya gacchasi.  || 354 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].9    Uyyānabhūmiṃ gantvāna pokkharaññā samantato, tassā tīre tuvaṃ ṭhāsi[^042-28] harite saddale subhe.  || 355 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].10   Tato te kaṇṇamuṇḍo[^042-29] sunakho aṅga-m-aṅgāni khādati; yadā ca khāyitā[^042-30] āsi aṭṭhisaṅkhalikā katā ogāhasi pokkharaṇiṃ hoti kāyo yathā pure.  || 356 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].11   Tato tuvaṃ aṅgapaccaṅgā[^042-31] sucārū[^042-32] piyadassanā vatthena pārupitvāna āyāsi mama santikaṃ.  || 357 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].12   Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ kissa kammavipākena kaṇṇamuṇḍo ca[^042-33] sunakho[^042-33] aṅga-m-aṅgāni khādatī ti.  || 358 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].13   Kimbilāyaṃ[^042-34] gahapati saddho āsi upāsako tassāhaṃ bhariyā āsiṃ[^042-35] dussīlā aticārinī.  || 359 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].14   Evaṃ[^042-36] aticaramānāya sāmiko etad abravi:[^042-37] n'; etaṃ channaṃ, na ppaṭirūpaṃ[^042-38] yaṃ tvaṃ aticarāsi maṃ.  || 360 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].15   Sāhaṃ ghorañ ca sapathaṃ musāvādaṃ abhāsisaṃ:[^042-39] nāhaṃ taṃ aticarāmi kāyena uda cetasā.  || 361 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].16   Sacāhaṃ taṃ aticarāmi kāyena uda cetasā kaṇṇamuṇḍo[^042-40] 'yaṃ[^042-40] sunakho aṅga-m-aṅgāni khādatu.  || 362 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].17   Tassa kammassa vipākaṃ musāvādassa cūbhayaṃ satt'; eva[^042-41] vassasatāni[^042-41] anubhūtaṃ yato pi[^042-42] me kaṇṇamuṇḍo ca[^042-43] sunakho aṅga-m-aṅgāni khādati.[^042-44]  || 363 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].18  Tvañ ca deva bahukāro[^042-45] atthāya me idhāgato sumuttāhaṃ kaṇṇamuṇḍassa asokā akutobhayā.  ||  
364 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 043 -->
<a id="page-043"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 043]*

Vv_II,12[=24].19   Tāhaṃ[^043-46] deva namassāmi yācāmi pañjalīkatā bhuñja amānuse kāme rama deva mayā sahā ti.[^043-47]  || 365 ||  
Vv_II,12[=24].20   Bhuttā[^043-48] amānusā kāmā ramito 'mhi tayā saha tāhaṃ subhage yācāmi khippaṃ paṭinayāhi man ti.  || 366 ||

25 Ubbaripetavatthu Vv_II,13[=25].1    Ahu rājā Brahmadatto Pañcālānaṃ rathesabho ahorattānam accayā rājā kālaṅkarī[^044-1] tadā.[^044-1]  || 367 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].2    Tassa āḷāhanaṃ gantvā bhariyā kandati Ubbarī Brahmadattaṃ apassantī Brahmadattā ti kandati.  || 368 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].3    Isi ca tattha āgacchi[^044-2] sampannacaraṇo[^044-3] muni so[^044-4] ca tattha apucchittha ye tattha su samāgatā:  || 369 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].4    Kassa c'[^044-5] idaṃ āḷāhanaṃ nānāgandhasameritaṃ kassāyaṃ kandati bhariyā ito dūragataṃ patiṃ, Brahmadattaṃ apassantī Brahmadattā ti kandati.  || 370 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].5    Te ca tattha viyākaṃsu ye tattha su samāgatā: Brahmadattassa bhaddan te,[^044-6] Brahmadattassa mārisa.  || 371 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].6    Tassa idaṃ āḷāhanaṃ nānāgandhasameritaṃ tassāyaṃ kandati bhariyā ito dūragataṃ patiṃ, Brahmadattaṃ apassantī Brahmadattā ti kandatī ti.[^044-7]  || 372 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].7    Chaḷāsīti sahassāni Brahmadatta ssanāmakā[^044-8] imasmiṃ āḷāhane daḍḍhā tesaṃ kam anusocasī ti.  || 373 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].8    Yo rājā Cūḷanīputto[^044-9] Pañcālānaṃ rathesabho taṃ bhante anusocāmi bhattāraṃ sabbakāmadaṃ.  || 374 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].9    Sabbe v'; ahesuṃ[^044-10] rājāno Brahmadatta ssanāmakā sabbe va Cūḷanīputtā[^044-9] Pañcālānaṃ rathesabhā.  ||  
375 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 045 -->
<a id="page-045"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 045]*

Vv_II,13[=25].10   Sabbesaṃ anupubbena mahesittam akārayi kasmā purimake hitvā pacchimaṃ anusocasī ti.  || 376 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].11   Ātume[^045-11] itthibhūtāya dīgharattāya mārisa yassā me itthibhūtāya saṃsāre bahu bhāsasī ti.  || 377 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].12  Ahu itthi[^045-12] ahu puriso pasuyonim pi[^045-13] āgamā evam etaṃ atītānaṃ[^045-14] pariyanto na dissatī ti.  || 378 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].13   Ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ ghatasittaṃ va pāvakaṃ vārinā viya osiñcaṃ[^045-15] sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ.  || 379 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].14   Abbūḷhaṃ[^045-16] vata me sallaṃ sokaṃ[^045-17] hadayanissitaṃ yo me sokaparetāya patisokaṃ apānudi.  || 380 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].15   Sāhaṃ abbūḷhasallāsmi sītibhūtāsmi nibbutā na socāmi na rodāmi tava sutvā mahāmunī ti.  || 381 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].16   Tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā samaṇassa subhāsitaṃ pattacīvaram ādāya pabbaji anagāriyaṃ.  || 382 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].17   Sā ca pabbajitā[^045-18] santā agārasmā[^045-19] anagāriyaṃ mettaṃ cittaṃ[^045-20] abhāvesi[^045-21] brahmalokūpapattiyā.[^045-22]  || 383 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].18   Gāmā gāmaṃ vicarantī nigame rājadhāniyo[^045-23] Uruvelā[^045-24] nāma so gāmo yattha kālam akubbatha.[^045-25]  || 384 ||  
Vv_II,13[=25].19   Mettacittam[^045-26] ābhāvetvā[^045-27] brahmalokūpapattiyā[^045-22] itthicittaṃ virājetvā brahmalokūpagā ahū ti.  || 385 ||  
Ubbaripetavatthu terasamaṃ Tass'; uddānaṃ: Paṇḍu[^045-28] mātā patiyā[^045-29] ca Nandā kuṇḍalinā[^045-30] Ghato dve seṭṭhī tunnavāyo ca vihāra[^045-31]-sutta-sopāna-[^045-32] Ubbarī ti. UBBARIVAGGA DUTIYO

III CŪḶAVAGGA 26 Abhijjamānapetavatthu Vv_III,1[=26].1    Abhijjamāne vārimhi Gaṅgaya idha gacchasi naggo pubbaddhapeto[^047-1] va mālādhārī[^047-2] alaṅkato, kuhiṃ gamissasi[^047-3] peta[^047-4] kattha vāso bhavissatī ti.  || 386 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].2    Cundatthikaṃ[^047-5] gamissāmi peto so iti bhāsati[^047-6] antare[^047-7] Vāsabhagāmaṃ Bārāṇasiyā[^047-8] ca[^047-9] santike.  || 387 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].3    Tañ ca disvā mahāmatto Koliyo iti vissuto sattubhattañ[^047-10] ca petassa pītakañ ca yugaṃ adā.  || 388 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].4    Nāvāya tiṭṭhamānāya kappakassa adapāyi kappakassa padinnamhi[^047-11] ṭhāne petass'; udissatha.[^047-12]  || 389 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].5    Tato suvatthavasano mālādhārī[^047-2] alaṅkato ṭhāne ṭhit'; assa petassa dakkhiṇā upakappatha; tasmā dajjetha petānaṃ anukampāya punappunan ti.  || 390 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].6    Sāhunnavasanā[^047-13] eke aññe kesanivāsanā[^047-14] petā bhattāya gacchanti pakkamanti disodisaṃ.  || 391 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].7    Dūre eke[^047-15] padhāvitvā aladdhā ca[^047-16] nivattare chātā pamucchitā bhantā[^047-17] bhūmiyaṃ paṭisumbhitā.[^047-18]  || 392 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].8    Keci[^047-19] tattha papatitā[^047-20] bhūmiyaṃ paṭisumbhitā[^047-18] pubbe akatakalyāṇā aggidaḍḍhā va ātape.  || 393 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].9    Mayaṃ[^047-21] pubbe pāpadhammā gharaṇī[^047-22] kulamātaro santesu deyyadhammesu dīpaṃ nākamha attano.  ||  
394 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 048 -->
<a id="page-048"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 048]*

Vv_III,1[=26].10   Pahūtaṃ annapānam pi[^048-23] api ssu[^048-24] avakirīyati sammaggate[^048-25] pabbajite na ca kiñ ci adamhase.  || 395 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].11   Akammakāmā alasā sādukāmā mahagghasā ālopapiṇḍadātāro pāṭiggahe paribhāsimhase.[^048-26]  || 396 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].12   Te gharā tā ca[^048-27] dāsiyo tān'; evābharaṇāni no te aññe[^048-28] paricārenti[^048-29] mayaṃ dukkhassa bhāgino.  || 397 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].13  Veṇī[^048-30] vā avaññā[^048-31] honti rathakārī ca dubbhikā caṇḍālī kapaṇā honti nhāminī[^048-32] ca punappunaṃ.  || 398 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].14   Yāni yāni nihīnāni kulāni kapaṇāni ca tesu tesv eva jāyanti esā maccharino gati.  || 399 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].15   Pubbe ca[^048-33] katakalyāṇā dāyakā vītamaccharā saggaṃ te paripūrenti obhāsenti ca Nandanaṃ.  || 400 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].16   Vejayante ca pāsāde ramitvā kāmakāmino uccākulesu jāyanti sabhogesu tato cutā.  || 401 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].17   Kūṭāgāre ca pāsāde pallaṅke goṇakatthate[^048-34] vījitaṅgā[^048-35] morahatthehi kule jātā yasassino.  || 402 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].18   Aṅkato[^048-36] aṅkaṃ[^048-36] gacchanti mālādhārī[^048-2] alaṅkatā dhātiyo[^048-37] upatiṭṭhanti sāyaṃ pātaṃ sukhesino.  || 403 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].19   Na-y-idaṃ akatapuññānaṃ katapuññānam ev'; idaṃ asokaṃ Nandanaṃ rammaṃ Tidasānaṃ mahāvanaṃ.  || 404 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].20   Sukhaṃ akatapuññānaṃ idha natthi parattha ca sukhañ ca katapuññānaṃ idha c'; eva parattha ca.  || 405 ||  
Vv_III,1[=26].21   Tesaṃ sahavyakāmānaṃ kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ bahuṃ Katapuññā hi modanti sagge bhogasamaṅgino ti.  || 406 ||  
Abhijjamānapetavatthu paṭhamaṃ

27 Sānuvāsipetavatthu Vv_III,2[=27].1    Kuṇḍināgariyo[^049-1] thero Sānuvāsinivāsiko[^049-2] Poṭṭhapādo ti nāmena samaṇo bhāvitindriyo.  || 407 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].2    Tassa mātā pitā bhātā duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokaṃ ito gatā.  || 408 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].3    Te duggatā sūcikaṭṭā[^049-3] kilantā naggino kisā uttasantā[^049-4] mahātāsā[^049-5] na dassenti[^049-6] kurūrino.[^049-7]  || 409 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].4    Tassa bhātā vitaritvā[^049-8] naggo ekapath'; ekako catukuṇḍiko bhavitvāna therassa dassayī[^049-9] tumaṃ.  || 410 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].5    Thero cāmanasī[^049-10]-katvā tuṇhībhūto apakkami[^049-11] so ca viññāpayī[^049-12] theraṃ:[^049-13] bhātā petagato[^049-14] ahaṃ.  ||  
411 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 050 -->
<a id="page-050"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 050]*

Vv_III,2[=27].6    Mātā pitā ca te bhante duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokaṃ ito gatā.  || 412 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].7    Te duggatā sūcikaṭṭā[^050-3] kilantā naggino kisā uttasantā[^050-4] mahātāsā[^050-5] na dassenti[^050-6] kurūrino.[^050-7]  || 413 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].8    Anukampassu kāruṇiko[^050-15] datvā anvādisāhi[^050-16] no teva dinnena dānena yāpessanti kurūrino.[^050-7]  || 414 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].9    Thero caritvā piṇḍāya bhikkhū aññe ca dvādasa ekajjhaṃ sannipatiṃsu bhattavissaggakāraṇā.[^050-17]  || 415 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].10   Thero sabbe va[^050-18] te āha: yathāladdhaṃ dadātha me saṅghabhattaṃ karissāmi anukampāya ñātinaṃ.[^050-19]  || 416 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].11   Niyyātayiṃsu[^050-20] therassa thero saṅghaṃ nimantayi datvā anvādisī[^050-21] thero mātu[^050-22] pitu[^050-22] ca bhātuno:[^050-23] idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu sukhitā honti ñātayo.  || 417 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].12   Samanantarānuddiṭṭhe[^050-24] bhojanaṃ upapajjatha[^050-25] suciṃ paṇītaṃ sampannaṃ anekarasavyañjanaṃ.  || 418 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].13   Tato uddissati[^050-26] bhātā vaṇṇavā balavā sukhī: pahūtaṃ bhojanaṃ bhante passa naggāmhase[^050-27] mayaṃ, tathā bhante parakkama[^050-28] yathā vatthaṃ labhāmase.[^050-29]  || 419 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].14   Thero saṅkārakūṭamhā[^050-30] uccinitvāna nantake[^050-31] pilotikaṃ paṭaṃ katvā saṅghe cātuddise adā.  || 420 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].15   Datvā anvādisī[^050-32] thero mātu[^050-22] pitu[^050-22] ca bhātuno: idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu sukhitā hontu ñātayo.  || 421 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].16   Samanantarānuddiṭṭhe[^050-24] vatthāni upapajjisuṃ[^050-33] tato suvatthavasano[^050-34] therassa dassayī[^050-35] tumaṃ.[^050-36]  || 422 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].17   [^050-37]Yāvatā Nandarājassa vijitasmiṃ paṭicchadā[^050-38] tato bahutarā[^050-39] bhante vatthān'; acchādanāni[^050-40] no.  || 423 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].18   Koseyyakambalīyāni khomakappāsikāni[^050-41] ca vipulā ca mahagghā ca te p'; ākāse[^050-42] 'valambare.  || 424 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].19   Te mayaṃ paridahāma yaṃ yaṃ hi manaso piyaṃ tathā bhante parakkama[^050-43] yathā gehaṃ[^050-44] labhāmase.  || 425 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].20   Thero paṇṇakuṭim katvā saṅghe catuddise adā datvā anvādisī[^050-45] thero mātu[^050-22] pitu[^050-22] ca bhātuno: idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu sukhitā hontu ñātayo.  ||  
426 ||

Vv_III,2[=27].21   Samanantarānuddiṭṭhe[^051-24] gharāni upapajjisuṃ[^051-46] kūṭāgāranivesanā[^051-47] vibhattā bhāgaso mitā.  || 427 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].22   Na manussesu īdisā yādisā no gharā idha api dibbesu yādisā tādisā no gharā idha.  || 428 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].23   Daddallamānā[^051-48] ābhenti[^051-49] samantā caturo disā tathā bhante parakkama[^051-28] yathā pānaṃ[^051-50] labhāmase.[^051-51]  || 429 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].24   Thero karakaṃ[^051-52] pūretvā saṅghe cātuddise adā datvā anvādisī[^051-53] thero mātu[^051-22] pitu[^051-22] ca bhātuno: idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu sukhitā hontu ñātayo.  || 430 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].25   Samanantarānuddiṭṭhe[^051-24] pānīyaṃ upapajjatha[^051-54] gambhīrā caturassā ca pokkharañño[^051-55] sunimmitā.[^051-56]  || 431 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].26   Sītodikā[^051-57] supatitthā[^051-58] sītā appaṭigandhiyā padumuppalasañchannā vārikiñjakkhapūritā.  || 432 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].27   Tattha nahātvā[^051-59] pivitvā ca[^051-60] therassa paṭidassayuṃ pahūtaṃ panīyaṃ bhante pādā[^051-61] dukkhā[^051-62] phalanti no.  || 433 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].28   Āhiṇḍamānā khañjāma sakkhare kusakaṇṭake tathā bhante parakkama[^051-28] yathā yānaṃ labhāmāse.  || 434 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].29   Thero sipāṭikaṃ laddhā saṅghe cātuddise adā datvā anvādisī[^051-53] thero mātu[^051-22] pitu[^051-22] ca bhātuno: idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu sukhitā hontu ñātayo.  || 435 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].30   Samanantarānuddiṭṭhe[^051-24] petā rathena-m-āgamuṃ anukampit'; amha bhaddan te[^051-63] bhatten' acchādanena[^051-64] ca.  || 436 ||  
Vv_III,2[=27].31   Gharena pānadānena[^051-65] yānadānena cūbhayaṃ muniṃ kāruṇikaṃ loke bhante[^051-66] vanditum āgatā ti.  || 437 ||

28 Rathakārapetavatthu Vv_III,3[=28].1        Veḷuriyatthambhaṃ ruciraṃ pabhassaraṃ vimānam āruyha anekacittaṃ tatth'; acchasi devi mahānubhāve pathaddhani[^053-1] paṇṇarase va cando.[^053-2]  || 438 ||  
Vv_III,3[=28].2        Vaṇṇo ca[^053-3] te kanakassa[^053-4] sannibho[^053-4] uttattarūpo[^053-5] bhusa dassanīyo pallaṅkaseṭṭhe atule nisinnā ekā tuvaṃ natthi ca[^053-6] tuyha[^053-6] sāmiko.  || 439 ||  
Vv_III,3[=28].3        Imā ca te pokkharañño[^053-7] samantā[^053-8] pahūtamalyā[^053-9] bahupuṇḍarīkā suvaṇṇacuṇṇehi[^053-10] samantam[^053-11] otthatā[^053-11] na tattha paṅko paṇako[^053-12] ca vijjati.  || 440 ||  
Vv_III,3[=28].4        Haṃsā c'; ime[^053-13] dassanīyā manoramā udakasmiṃ anupariyanti sabbadā samayya vaggūpanadanti[^053-14] sabbe bindussarā[^053-15] dundubhīnaṃ va ghoso.  || 441 ||  
Vv_III,3[=28].5        Daddallamānā[^053-16] yasasā yasassinī nāvāya ca[^053-17] tvaṃ avalamba tiṭṭhasi āḷārapamhe[^053-18] hasite piyaṃvade sabbaṅgakalyāṇi bhusaṃ virocasi.  ||  
442 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 054 -->
<a id="page-054"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 054]*

Vv_III,3[=28].6        Idaṃ vimānaṃ virajaṃ same ṭhitaṃ uyyānavantaṃ[^054-19] ratinandivaḍḍhanaṃ[^054-20] icchām'; ahaṃ[^054-21] nāri anomadassane tayā saha Nandane idha moditun ti.  || 443 ||  
Vv_III,3[=28].7        Karohi kammaṃ idha vedanīyaṃ cittañ ca te idha nihitaṃ[^054-22] bhavatu[^054-22] katvāna kammaṃ idha vedanīyaṃ[^054-23] evaṃ mamaṃ lacchasi kāmakāminiṃ.  || 444 ||  
Vv_III,3[=28].8        Sādhū ti so tassā paṭissuṇitvā[^054-24] akāsi kammaṃ sahavedanīyaṃ[^054-25] katvāna kammaṃ tahiṃ vedanīyaṃ uppajji[^054-26] māṇavo tassā sahavyatan ti.  || 445 ||  
Rathakārapetavatthu[^054-27] tatiyaṃ BHĀṆAVĀRAṂ DUTIYAṂ

29 Bhusapetavatthu Vv_III,4[=29].1        Bhusāni eko[^055-1] sāliṃ punāparo[^055-1] ayañ[^055-2] ca[^055-2] nārī sakamaṃsalohitaṃ tuvañ ca gūthaṃ asuciṃ[^055-3] akantikaṃ[^055-3] paribhuñjasi kissa ayaṃ vipāko ti.  || 446 ||  
Vv_III,4[=29].2    Ayaṃ pure mātaraṃ hiṃsati ayaṃ pana kūṭavāṇijo ayaṃ maṃsāni khāditvā musāvadena vañceti.[^055-4]  || 447 ||  
Vv_III,4[=29].3        Ahaṃ manussesu manussabhūtā agārinī sabbakulassa[^055-5] issarā santesu pariguyhāmi:[^055-6] mā ca kiñ ci ito adaṃ.  || 448 ||  
Vv_III,4[=29].4    Musāvādena chādemi: natthi etaṃ mama[^055-7] gehe[^055-7] sace santaṃ[^055-8] niguyhāmi[^055-9] gūtho me hotu bhojanaṃ.  || 449 ||  
Vv_III,4[=29].5    Tassa kammassa[^055-10] vipākena musāvādassa cūbhayaṃ sugandhasālino[^055-11] bhattaṃ gūthaṃ me parivattati.  || 450 ||  
Vv_III,4[=29].6    Avañjhāni[^055-12] ca kammāni na hi kammaṃ vinassati duggandhaṃ kimijaṃ[^055-13] mīḷhaṃ bhuñjāmi ca pivāmi cā ti.  || 451 ||  
Bhusapetavatthu catutthaṃ &1 30 Kumārapetavatthu Vv_III,5[=30].1        Accherarūpam sugatassa ñāṇaṃ satthā yathā puggalaṃ vyākāsi ussannapuññā pi bhavanti h'; eke[^055-1] parittapuññā pi bhavanti h'; eke.[^055-1]  ||  
452 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 056 -->
<a id="page-056"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 056]*

Vv_III,5[=30].2        Ayaṃ[^056-2] kumāro sīvathikāya chaḍḍito aṅguṭṭhasnehena yāpeti[^056-3] rattiṃ[^056-4] na yakkhabhūtā na siriṃsapā[^056-5] vā viheṭhayeyyuṃ[^056-6] katapuññakumāraṃ.[^056-7]  || 453 ||  
Vv_III,5[=30].3        Sunakhā p'; imassa[^056-8] palihiṃsu[^056-9] pāde dhaṅkā sigālā[^056-10] parivattayanti gabbhāsayaṃ pakkhigaṇā haranti kākā pana akkhimalaṃ haranti.  || 454 ||  
Vv_III,5[=30].4        Na-y-imassa[^056-11] rakkhaṃ vidahiṃsu keci na osadhaṃ[^056-12] sāsapadhūpanaṃ vā nakkhattayogam pi na aggahesuṃ[^056-13] na sabbadhaññāni pi ākiriṃsu.  || 455 ||  
Vv_III,5[=30].5        Etādisaṃ uttamakicchapattaṃ[^056-14] rattābhataṃ sīvathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ nonītapiṇḍaṃ[^056-15] va[^056-16] pavedhamānaṃ[^056-16] sasaṃsayaṃ jīvitasāvasesaṃ.  || 456 ||  
Vv_III,5[=30].6        Tam addasā[^056-17] devamanussapūjito disvā va[^056-18] taṃ vyākari bhūripañño: ayaṃ kumāro nagarass'; imassa aggakuliko bhavissati bhogato[^056-19] ca.  || 457 ||  
Vv_III,5[=30].7        Ki 'ssa vataṃ kiṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ kissa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipāko etādisaṃ vyasanaṃ pāpuṇitvā taṃ tādisaṃ paccanubhossat'; iddhiṃ.  || 458 ||  
Vv_III,5[=30].8        Buddhapamukhassa[^056-20] bhikkhusaṅghassa[^056-21] pūjaṃ akāsi janatā uḷāraṃ tatr'; assa[^056-22] cittass'; ahu[^056-23] aññathattaṃ vācaṃ abhāsi pharusaṃ asabbhaṃ.[^056-24]  || 459 ||  
Vv_III,5[=30].9        So taṃ vitakkaṃ pavinodayitvā[^056-25] pītiṃ[^056-26] pasādaṃ[^056-26] paṭiladdhā[^056-27] pacchā tathāgataṃ Jetavane vasantaṃ yāguyā upaṭṭhāsi[^056-28] sattarattaṃ.  || 460 ||  
Vv_III,5[=30].10       Tassa vataṃ taṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ tassa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipāko etādisaṃ vyasanaṃ pāpuṇitvā taṃ tādisaṃ paccanubhossat'; iddhiṃ.  || 461 ||  
Vv_III,5[=30].11       Ṭhatvāna so vassasataṃ idh'; eva sabbehi kāmehi samaṅgibhūto kāyassa bhedā abhisamparāyaṃ sahavyataṃ gacchati Vāsavassā ti.  || 462 ||  
Kumārapetavatthu pañcamaṃ

31 Seriṇīpetavatthu Vv_III,6[=31].1    Naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi kisā[^057-1] dhamanisanthatā upphāsulike kisike[^057-2] kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasī ti.  || 463 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].2    Ahaṃ bhadante petī 'mhi duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokam ito gatā.  || 464 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].3    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ kissa kammavipākena petalokam ito gatā ti.  || 465 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].4    Anāvaṭesu[^057-3] titthesu viciniṃ[^057-4] addhamāsakaṃ[^057-5] santesu deyyadhammesu dīpaṃ nākāsim attano.  || 466 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].5    Nadiṃ upemi tasitā rittakā parivattati chāyaṃ upemi uṇhesu ātapo parivattati.  || 467 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].6    Aggivaṇṇo ca me vāto dahanto[^057-6] upavāyati etañ ca bhante arahāmi aññañ ca pāpakaṃ tato.  ||  
468 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 058 -->

##### *[PTS Page 058]*

Vv_III,6[=31].7    Gantvāna Hatthinipuraṃ[^058-7] vajjesi mayha mātaraṃ dhītā ca te mayā diṭṭhā duggatā Yamalokikā, pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokaṃ ito gatā.  || 469 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].8    Atthi ca[^058-8] me ettha nikkhittaṃ anakkhātañ ca taṃ mayā cattāri satasahassāni pallaṅkassa ca heṭṭhato.  || 470 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].9    Tato me dānaṃ dadatu[^058-9] tassā ca hotu jīvikā dānaṃ datvā[^058-10] ca[^058-10] me mātā dakkhiṇaṃ anvādissatu,[^058-11] tadāhaṃ sukhitā hessaṃ sabbakāmasamiddhinī ti.  || 471 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].10   Sādhū ti so[^058-12] paṭissutvā[^058-13] gantvāna Hatthiniṃ[^058-14] puraṃ[^058-14] -- avoca[^058-15] tassā[^058-15] mātaraṃ: dhītā ca te mayā diṭṭhā duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ paritvāna petalokaṃ ito gatā.  || 472 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].11   Sā maṃ tattha samādapesi gantvāna[^058-16] Hatthiniṃ[^058-17] puraṃ -- vajjesi mayha mātaraṃ: dhītā ca te mayā diṭṭhā duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokaṃ ito gatā.  || 473 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].12   Atthi ca[^058-18] me ettha[^058-19] nikkhittaṃ anakkhātañ ca taṃ mayā[^058-20] cattārī satasahassāni pallaṅkassa ca heṭṭhato.  || 474 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].13   Tato me dānaṃ dadatu tassā ca hotu jīvikā dānaṃ datvā ca me mātā dakkhiṇaṃ anvādissatu,[^058-21] tadāhaṃ sukhitā hessaṃ sabbakāmasamiddhinī ti.  || 475 ||  
Vv_III,6[=31].14   Tato hi sā dānam adā[^058-22] tassā[^058-23] ca[^058-23] dakkhiṇam ādisi petī ca sukhitā āsi sarīraṃ[^058-24] cārudassanan[^058-24] ti.  || 476 ||  
Seriṇīpetavatthu chaṭṭhaṃ

32 Migaluddapetavatthu Vv_III,7[=32].1    Naranāripurakkhato[^059-1] yuvā rajanīyehi[^059-2] kāmaguṇehi[^059-2] sobhasi divasaṃ anubhosi kāraṇaṃ kim akāsi purimāya jātiyā ti.  || 477 ||  
Vv_III,7[=32].2    Ahaṃ Rājagahe ramme ramaṇīye Giribbaje migaluddo pure āsiṃ lohitapāṇi[^059-3] dāruṇo.  || 478 ||  
Vv_III,7[=32].3    Avirodhakaresu pāṇisu[^059-4] puthusattesu[^059-5] paduṭṭhamānaso vicariṃ[^059-6] atidāruṇo sadā[^059-7] parahiṃsāya rato asaññato.[^059-8]  || 479 ||  
Vv_III,7[=32].4    Tassa me sahāyo suhadayo[^059-9] saddho āsi upāsako so ca[^059-10] maṃ anukampanto nivāresi punappunaṃ:  || 480 ||  
Vv_III,7[=32].5    Mākāsi pāpakaṃ kammaṃ mā tāta[^059-11] duggatiṃ agā sace icchasi pecca sukhaṃ virama[^059-12] pāṇavadhā[^059-13] asaṃyamā.[^059-13]  || 481 ||  
Vv_III,7[=32].6        Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā sukhakāmassa hitānukampino nākāsiṃ sakalānusāsaniṃ cirapāpābhirato[^059-14] abuddhimā.  || 482 ||  
Vv_III,7[=32].7        So maṃ puna bhūrisumedhaso anukampāya saṃyame nivesayi: sace divā hanasi pāṇino atha te rattiṃ bhavatu[^059-15] saññamo.[^059-16]  ||  
483 ||

Vv_III,7[=32].9    Tassa kammassa kusalassa anubhomi rattiṃ amānusiṃ divā paṭihatā va[^060-22] kukkurā upadhāvanti samantā khādituṃ.  || 485 ||  
Vv_III,7[=32].10       Ye ca te satatānuyogino[^060-23] dhuvaṃ payuttā sugatassa sāsane maññāmi te amatam eva kevalaṃ adhigacchanti padaṃ asaṅkhatan ti.  || 486 ||  
Migaluddapetavatthu sattamaṃ &1 33 Dutiyamigaluddapetavatthu Vv_III,8[=33].1    Kūṭāgāre ca pāsāde pallaṅke goṇakatthate[^060-1] pañcaṅgikena turiyena ramasi suppavādite.  || 487 ||

Vv_III,8[=33].9        Svāhaṃ divā hanitva[^061-13] pāṇino virato rattim ahosi[^061-14] saññato[^061-15] rattāhaṃ paricāremi[^061-16] divā khajjāmi duggato.  || 495 ||

34 Kūṭavinicchayikapetavatthu Vv_III,9[=34].1    Mālī kirīṭī[^062-1] kāyūrī[^062-2] gattā te candanussadā pasannamukhavaṇṇo 'si suriyavaṇṇo[^062-3] va sobhasi.  || 498 ||  
Vv_III,9[=34].2    Amānusā pārisajjā[^062-4] ye te 'me paricārakā[^062-5] dasa kaññāsahassāni yā te 'mā paricārikā, tā[^062-6] kambukāyūradharā[^062-7] kañcanāveḷabhūsitā.[^062-8]  || 499 ||  
Vv_III,9[=34].3    Mahānubhāvo 'si tuvaṃ lomahaṃsanarūpavā, piṭṭhimaṃsāni attano sāmaṃ ukkacca[^062-9] khādasi.  || 500 ||  
Vv_III,9[=34].4    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ kissa kammavipākena piṭṭhimaṃsāni attano sāmaṃ ukkacca[^062-9] khādasi.  || 501 ||  
Vv_III,9[=34].5    Attano 'haṃ anatthāya jīvaloke acārisaṃ[^062-10] pesuññamusāvādena nikativañcanāya ca.  || 502 ||  
Vv_III,9[=34].6    Tatthāhaṃ parisaṃ gantvā saccakāle upaṭṭhite atthaṃ dhammaṃ niraṃkatvā[^062-11] adhammaṃ anuvattisaṃ.[^062-12]  || 503 ||  
Vv_III,9[=34].7    Evaṃ so khādat'; attānaṃ[^062-13] yo hoti piṭṭhimaṃsiko[^062-14] yathāhaṃ ajja khādāmi piṭṭhimaṃsāni attano.  || 504 ||  
Vv_III,9[=34].8        Ta-y-idaṃ tayā Nārada sāmaṃ[^062-15] diṭṭhaṃ anukampakā ye kusalā vadeyyuṃ mā pesuṇaṃ mā ca musā abhāṇi[^062-16] mā kho 'si piṭṭhimaṃsiko tuvan ti.[^062-17]  || 505 ||  
Kūṭavinicchayika[^062-18]-petavatthu navamaṃ

35 Dhātuvivaṇṇakapetavatthu Vv_III,10[=35].1    Antalikkhasmiṃ[^063-1] tiṭṭhanto duggandho pūti vāyasi[^063-2] mukhañ ca te kimayo[^063-3] pūtigandhaṃ khādanti, kiṃ kammam akāsi pubbe.  || 506 ||  
Vv_III,10[=35].2    Tato[^063-4] satthaṃ[^063-4] gahetvāna okkantanti[^063-5] punappunaṃ khārena paripphositvā okkantanti[^063-6] punappunaṃ.  || 507 ||  
Vv_III,10[=35].3    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ kissa kammavipākena idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasi.  || 508 ||  
Vv_III,10[=35].4    Ahaṃ Rājagahe ramme ramaṇīye Giribbaje issaro dhanadhaññassa supahūtassa mārisa.  || 509 ||  
Vv_III,10[=35].5    Tassāyaṃ me bhariyā ca[^063-7] dhītā ca suṇisā ca me tā[^063-8] mālam[^063-8] uppalañ cāpi paccagghañ ca vilepanaṃ thūpaṃ harantiyo vāresiṃ taṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ mayā.  || 510 ||  
Vv_III,10[=35].6    Chaḷāsītisahassāni mayaṃ paccattavedanā thūpapūjaṃ vivaṇṇetvā paccāma[^063-9] niraye bhusaṃ.  || 511 ||  
Vv_III,10[=35].7    Ye ca kho thūpapūjāya vattante arahato mahe ādīnavaṃ pakāsenti, vivecayetha[^063-10] ne[^063-11] tato.  || 512 ||  
Vv_III,10[=35].8    Imā ca passa āyantiyo māladhārī[^063-12] alaṅkatā mālāvipākaṃ anubhontiyo[^063-13] samiddhā tā yasassiniyo.  || 513 ||  
Vv_III,10[=35].9    Tañ ca disvāna accheraṃ abbhutaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ namo karonti sappañña vandanti taṃ mahāmuniṃ.  ||  
514 ||

Vv_III,10[=35].10   So hi[^064-14] nūna[^064-14] ito gantvā yoniṃ laddhāna mānusiṃ thūpapūjaṃ karissāmi appamatto punappunan ti.  || 515 ||  
Dhātuvivaṇṇakapetavatthu dasamaṃ Tass'; uddānaṃ:[^064-15] Abhijjamāno Kuṇḍiyo[^064-16] rathakārī bhusena ca kumāro gaṇikā[^064-17] c'; eva dve luddā[^064-18] piṭṭh'; apūjayo;[^064-19] vaggo tena pavaccatī[^064-20] ti. CŪḶAVAGGO TATIYO.

IV MAHĀVAGGA 36 Ambasakkharapetavatthu Vv_IV,1[=36].1        Vesāli[^065-1] nāma nagar'; atthi Vajjinaṃ[^065-2] tattha ahu Licchavi Ambasakkharo[^065-3] disvāna petaṃ nagarassa bāhiraṃ tatth'; eva pucchittha taṃ kāraṇatthiko.  || 516 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].2        Seyyā[^065-4] nisajjā na-y-imassa atthi abhikkamo natthi paṭikkamo vā[^065-5] asitapītakhāyitavatthabhogā[^065-6] paricāraṇā[^065-7] sā pi imassa[^065-8] natthi.  || 517 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].3        Ye ñātakā diṭṭhasutā suhajjā anukampakā yassa ahesuṃ pubbe daṭṭhum pi te[^065-9] dāni na taṃ[^065-10] labhanti[^065-10] virādhitatto[^065-11] hi janena tena.  || 518 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].4        Na oggatattassa[^065-12] bhavanti mittā jahanti mittā vikalaṃ viditvā atthañ ca disvā parivārayanti[^065-13] bahū ca[^065-14] mittā uggatattassa[^065-15] honti.  || 519 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].5        Nihīnatto[^065-16] sabbabhogehi kiccho[^065-17] sammakkhito[^065-18] samparibhinnagatto ussāvabindū va[^065-19] palimpamāno ajja suve jīvitassūparodho.[^065-21]  || 520 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].6        Etādisaṃ uttamakicchapattaṃ[^065-22] uttāsitaṃ pucimandassa[^065-23] sūle atha tvaṃ kena vaṇṇena vadesi yakkha jīva bho jīvitam eva seyyo ti.  ||  
521 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 066 -->
<a id="page-066"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 066]*

Vv_IV,1[=36].7        Sālohito esa[^066-24] ahosi mayhaṃ ahaṃ sarāmi purimāya jātiyā disvā ca[^066-25] me kāruññam ahosi rāja[^066-26] mā pāpadhammo nirayaṃ patāyaṃ.  || 522 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].8       Ito cuto Licchavi esa[^066-24] poso sattussadaṃ nirayaṃ[^066-27] ghorarūpaṃ uppajjati dukkaṭakammakārī[^066-28] mahābhitāpaṃ kaṭukaṃ bhayānakaṃ.  || 523 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].9        Anekabhāgena guṇena seyyo ayam eva sūlo nirayena tena mā[^066-29] ekantadukkhaṃ kaṭukaṃ bhayānakaṃ ekantatippaṃ[^066-30] nirayaṃ patāyaṃ.[^066-31]  || 524 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].10       Idañ ca sutvā vacanaṃ mam'; eso dukkhūpanīto vijaheyya pāṇaṃ tasmā ahaṃ santike na bhaṇāmi mā m'; ekato[^066-32] jīvitassūparodho[^066-33] ti.  || 525 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].11       Aññāto[^066-34] eso[^066-34] purisassa attho aññam[^066-35] pi icchāmase pucchituṃ tuvaṃ okāsakammaṃ[^066-36] sace no karosi[^066-36] pucchāmi[^066-37] taṃ[^066-38] na[^066-39] ca no[^066-39] kujjhitabbaṃ.  || 526 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].12       Addhā paṭiñña[^066-40] me tadā ahu[^066-40] nācikkhanā[^066-41] appasannassa hoti akāmā saddheyyavaco ti katvā pucchassu maṃ kāmaṃ yathā visayhan[^066-42] ti.  || 527 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].13       Yaṃ kiñcāhaṃ cakkhunā passissāmi[^066-43] sabbam pi tāhaṃ abhisaddaheyyaṃ disvāna[^066-44] taṃ no pi ce saddaheyyaṃ[^066-45] kareyyāsi me yakkha niyassakamman[^066-46] ti.  || 528 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].14       Saccappaṭiññā[^066-47] tava m'; esā hotu sutvāna dhammaṃ labhassu[^066-48] pasādaṃ[^066-48] aññatthiko no ca paduṭṭhacitto yan te sutaṃ asutaṃ cāpi[^066-49] dhammaṃ sabbaṃ akkhissaṃ[^066-50] yathā pajānaṃ:  || 529 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].15       Setena assena alaṅkatena upayāsi sūlāvutakassa santike yānam idaṃ abbhutaṃ dassaneyyaṃ kiss'; etaṃ kammassa ayaṃ vipāko.  ||  
530 ||

Vv_IV,1[=36].16       Vesāliyā tassa[^067-51] nagarassa majjhe cikkhallamagge[^067-52] narakaṃ[^067-53] ahosi gosīsaṃ[^067-54] ekāhaṃ pasannacitto setaṃ[^067-55] gahetvāna narakasmiṃ nikkhipiṃ.[^067-56]  || 531 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].17       Etasmiṃ pādāni patiṭṭhapetvā mayañ ca aññe[^067-57] ca atikkamimha[^067-58] yānam idaṃ abbhutaṃ dassaneyyaṃ tass'; eva kammassa ayaṃ vipāko ti.  || 532 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].18       Vaṇṇo ca te sabbadisā pabhāsati gandho ca te sabbadisā pavāyati yakkhiddhipatto 'si mahānubhāvo naggo cāsi[^067-59] kissa ayaṃ vipāko.  || 533 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].19       Akkodhano niccapasannacitto[^067-60] saṇhāhi vācāhi janaṃ lapemi[^067-61] tass'; eva kammassa ayaṃ vipāko dibbo ca me vaṇṇo satataṃ pabhāsati.  || 534 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].20       Yasañ ca kittiñ ca dhamme ṭhitānaṃ disvāna mantemi pasannacitto tass'; eva kammassa ayaṃ vipāko dibbo me gandho satataṃ pavāyati.  || 535 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].21       Sahāyānaṃ titthasmiṃ[^067-62] nahāyantānaṃ[^067-63] thale gahetvā nidahissa dussaṃ khiḍḍatthiko[^067-64] no ca paduṭṭhacitto ten'; amhi naggo kasirā ca[^067-65] vutti.[^067-65]  || 536 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].22       Yo kīḷamāno pakaroti[^067-66] pāpaṃ tass'; īdisaṃ[^067-67] kammavipākam āhu akīḷamāno pana yo karoti kiṃ tassa kammassa vipākam āhu.  || 537 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].23       Ye duṭṭhasaṅkappamanā manussā kāyena vācāya ca saṅkiliṭṭhā kāyassa bhedā abhisamparāyaṃ asaṃsayaṃ te nirayaṃ upenti.  || 538 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].24       Apare pana sugatiṃ āsamānā[^067-68] dāne ratā saṅgahitattabhāvā[^067-69] kāyassa bhedā abhisamparāyaṃ asaṃsayaṃ te sugatiṃ upenti.  || 539 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].25       Taṃ kin ti jāneyyam ahaṃ avecca kalyāṇapāpassa ayaṃ vipāko kiṃ vāhaṃ disvā abhisaddaheyyaṃ ko vā pi maṃ saddahāpeyya etaṃ.  ||  
540 ||

Vv_IV,1[=36].26       Disvā ca sutvā abhisaddahassu kalyāṇapāpassa ayaṃ vipāko kalyāṇapāpe ubhaye asante siyā nu sattā sugatā duggatā vā.  || 541 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].27       No c'; ettha kammāni kareyyuṃ[^068-70] maccā kalyāṇapāpāni manussaloke tasmā hi sattā sugatā duggatā vā hīnā[^068-71] paṇītā[^068-71] ca manussaloke.  || 542 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].28       Yasmā ca kammāni karonti maccā kalyāṇapāpāni manussaloke tasmā hi[^068-72] sattā sugatā duggatā vā hīnā[^068-71] paṇītā[^068-71] ca manussaloke.  || 543 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].29       Dvay'; ajja[^068-73] kammānaṃ vipākam āhu sukhassa dukkhassa ca vedanīyaṃ tā[^068-74] devatāyo[^068-74] paricārayanti[^068-75] paccanti[^068-76] bālā dvyataṃ apassino.  || 544 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].30       Na m'; atthi kammāni sayaṃ katāni datvā pi me natthi so[^068-77] ādiseyya[^068-78] acchādanaṃ sayanam ath'; annapānaṃ ten'; amhi naggo kasirā ca[^068-65] vutti.[^068-65]  || 545 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].31       Siyā nu kho kāraṇaṃ kiñci yakkha acchādanaṃ yena tuvaṃ labhetha ācikkha me taṃ[^068-79] yad atthi hetu saddhāyitaṃ[^068-80] hetuvaco suṇoma.[^068-81]  || 546 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].32       Kappitako[^068-82] nāma idh'; atthi bhikkhu jhāyī susīlo arahā[^068-83] vimutto guttindriyo saṃvutapātimokkho sītibhūto uttamadiṭṭhipatto.  || 547 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].33       Sakhilo vadaññū suvaco sumukho[^068-84] svāgamo suppaṭimuttako ca[^068-85] puññassa khettaṃ araṇavihārī devamanussānañ ca dakkhiṇeyyo.  || 548 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].34       Santo vidhūmo anīgho nirāso mutto visallo amamo avaṅko nirūpadhī[^068-86] sabbapapañcakhīṇo tisso vijjā anuppatto jutīmā.[^068-87]  || 549 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].35       Appaññāto[^068-88] disvā pi na[^068-89] sujāno munī ti[^068-90] naṃ Vajjisu[^068-91] voharanti jānanti taṃ yakkhabhūtā[^068-92] anejaṃ kalyāṇadhammaṃ vicarantaṃ[^068-93] loke.  ||  
550 ||
