##### *[PTS Page 069]*

Vv_IV,1[=36].36       Tassa tuvaṃ ekaṃ[^069-94] yugaṃ[^069-94] duve vā mam'; uddisitvāna sace dadetha paṭiggahītāni[^069-95] ca tāni assu[^069-96] mamañ ca passetha sannaddhadussaṃ.  || 551 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].37       Kasmiṃ padese samaṇaṃ vasantaṃ gantvāna passemu mayaṃ idāni sa-m-ajja[^069-97] kaṅkhaṃ vicikicchitañ ca diṭṭhivisūkāni vinodaye[^069-98] me.[^069-98]  || 552 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].38       Eso nisinno Kapinaccanāyaṃ[^069-99] parivārito devatāhi bahūhi dhammiṃ[^069-100] kathaṃ bhāsati saccanāmo sakasmim ācerake[^069-101] appamatto.  || 553 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].39       Tathāhaṃ[^069-102] kassāmi gantvā idāni acchādayissaṃ samaṇaṃ yugena paṭiggahītāni ca tāni assu[^069-103] tuvañ ca passemu sannaddhadussaṃ.  || 554 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].40       Mā akkhaṇe pabbajitaṃ upāgami sādhu vo Licchavi n'; esa dhammo tato ca kāle upasaṅkamitvā tatth'; eva passāhi[^069-104] raho nisinnaṃ.  || 555 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].41       Tathā ti[^069-105] vatvā agamāsi tattha parivārito dāsagaṇena Licchavi so taṃ nagaraṃ upasaṅkamitvā vāsūpagacchittha[^069-106] sake nivesane.  || 556 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].42       Tato ca kāle gihikiccāni katvā nahātvā[^069-107] pivitvā ca khaṇaṃ labhitvā viceyya peḷāto[^069-108] ca yugāni aṭṭha gāhāpayī[^069-109] dāsagaṇena Licchavi.  || 557 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].43       So taṃ padesaṃ upasaṅkamitvā tam addasā[^069-110] samaṇaṃ santacittaṃ paṭikkantaṃ[^069-111] gocarato nivattaṃ sītibhūtaṃ rukkhamūle nisinnaṃ.  || 558 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].44       Tam enaṃ avoca upasaṅkamitvā appābādhaṃ phāsuvihārañ ca pucchi Vesāliyaṃ Licchavi 'haṃ[^069-112] bhadante[^069-113] jānanti maṃ Licchavi Ambasakkharo.  || 559 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].45       Imāni me aṭṭha yugā[^069-114] subhāni[^069-114] paṭigaṇha[^069-115] bhante padadāmi[^069-116] tuyhaṃ ten'; eva atthena idhāgato 'smi[^069-117] yathā ahaṃ attamano bhaveyyaṃ.  ||  
560 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 070 -->
<a id="page-070"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 070]*

Vv_IV,1[=36].46       Dūrato va samaṇā[^070-118] brāhmaṇā ca[^070-118] nivesanaṃ te parivajjayanti pattāni bhijjanti ca[^070-119] te[^070-119] nivesane saṅghāṭiyo cāpi[^070-120] vidālayanti.[^070-121]  || 561 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].47       Athāpare[^070-122] pādakuṭhārikāhi[^070-123] avaṃsirā samaṇā pātayanti[^070-124] etādisaṃ pabbajitā[^070-125]-vihesaṃ tayā kataṃ samaṇā pāpuṇanti  || 562 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].48       Tiṇena telam[^070-126] pi na tvaṃ adāsi mūḷhassa maggam pi na pāvadāsi andhassa daṇḍaṃ sayam ādiyāsi etādiso kadariyo asaṃvuto,[^070-127] atha[^070-128] tvaṃ kena vaṇṇena kim eva disvā amhehi saha saṃvibhāgaṃ karosi.  || 563 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].49       Paccemi bhante yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi vihesayiṃ[^070-129] samaṇabrāhmaṇe[^070-130] ca[^070-130] khiḍḍatthiko no ca paduṭṭhacitto etam pi me dukkaṭam eva bhante.  || 564 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].50       Khiḍḍāya yakkho[^070-131] pasavitva[^070-132] pāpaṃ vedeti dukkhaṃ asamattabhogī[^070-133] daharo yuvā nagganiyassa[^070-134] bhāgī kiṃ su tato dukkhatar'; assa hoti.  || 565 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].51       Taṃ disvā saṃvegam[^070-135] alatthaṃ[^070-135] bhante tappaccayā tāhaṃ[^070-136] dadāmi dānaṃ paṭigaṇha bhante vatthayugāni aṭṭha yakkhass'; imā[^070-137] gacchantu[^070-137] dakkhiṇāyo.  || 566 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].52       Addhā[^070-138] hi[^070-138] dānaṃ bahudhā pasatthaṃ[^070-139] dadato ca te akkhayadhammam atthu paṭigaṇhāmi[^070-140] te vatthayugāni aṭṭha yakkhass'; imā[^070-137] gacchantu[^070-137] dakkhiṇāyo.  || 567 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].53       Tato hi[^070-141] so ācamayitva[^070-142] Licchavi therassa datvāna yugāni aṭṭha paṭiggahītāni[^070-143] ca[^070-144] tāni c'; assu[^070-144] yakkhañ ca passetha sannaddhadussaṃ.  || 568 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].54       Tam addasā[^070-145] candanasāralittaṃ ājaññam ārūḷham[^070-146] uḷāravaṇṇaṃ alaṅkataṃ sādhunivatthadussaṃ parivāritaṃ yakkhamahiddhipattaṃ.  ||  
569 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 071 -->
<a id="page-071"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 071]*

Vv_IV,1[=36].55       So taṃ disvā attamano udaggo pahaṭṭhacitto ca[^071-147] subhaggarūpo kammañ ca disvāna mahāvipākaṃ sandiṭṭhikaṃ cakkhunā sacchikatvā.  || 570 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].56       Tam enaṃ avoca upasaṅkamitvā: dassāmi dānaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ na cāpi me kiñ ci adeyyam atthi tuvañ ca me yakkha bahūpakāro.  || 571 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].57       Tuvañ ca me Licchavi ekadesaṃ adāsi dānāni amogham etaṃ svāhaṃ karissāmi tayā va[^071-148] sakkhiṃ amānuso mānusakena saddhiṃ.  || 572 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].58       Gatī[^071-149] ca bandhū[^071-150] ca[^071-151] parāyaṇañ[^071-152] ca mitto mamāsi[^071-153] atha devatā si[^071-154] yācām'; ahaṃ[^071-155] pañjaliko bhavitvā icchāmi taṃ yakkha punāpi daṭṭhuṃ.  || 573 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].59       Sace tuvaṃ assaddho bhavissasi kadariyarūpo vippaṭipannacitto ten'; eva[^071-156] maṃ na[^071-157] lacchasi[^071-158] dassanāya disvā ca taṃ nāpi[^071-159] ca ālapissaṃ.  || 574 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].60       Sace[^071-160] tvaṃ bhavissasi dhammagāravo dāne rato saṅgahitattabhāvo[^071-161] opānabhūto samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ evaṃ mamaṃ lacchasi[^071-158] dassanāya.  || 575 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].61       Disvā ca taṃ ālapissaṃ bhadante[^071-162] imañ ca sūlato lahuṃ[^071-163] pamuñca yatonidānaṃ akariṃha sakkhiṃ maññāmi[^071-164] sūlāvutakassa kāraṇā.  || 576 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].62       Te[^071-165] {aññamaññaṃ} akarimha sakkhiṃ ayañ ca sūlāvuto[^071-166] lahuṃ pamutto sakkacca dhammāni samācaranto mucceyya[^071-167] so nirayā ca[^071-168] tamhā.  || 577 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].63       Kammaṃ siyā aññatra vedanīyaṃ[^071-169] Kappitakañ ca[^071-170] upasaṅkamitvā ten'; eva[^071-171] saha samvibhajitva[^071-172] kāle sayam mukhen'; upanisajja[^071-173] puccha.  || 578 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].64       So te akkhissati etam atthaṃ tam eva bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchassu aññatthiko[^071-174] no[^071-175] ca[^071-175] paduṭṭhacitto; so te[^071-176] sutaṃ[^071-176] asutañ cāpi[^071-177] dhammaṃ[^071-178]


<!-- PTS Page 072 -->
<a id="page-072"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 072]*

sabbam pi[^072-178] akkhissati yathā pajānaṃ: suto ca dhammaṃ sugatiṃ akkhissa.[^072-179]  || 579 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].65       So tattha rahassaṃ samullapitvā sakkhiṃ karitvāna[^072-180] amānusena pakkāmi so Licchavīnaṃ sakāsaṃ ath'; abravī[^072-181] parisaṃ sannisinnaṃ:  || 580 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].66       Suṇantu bhonto mama ekavākyaṃ varaṃ varissaṃ labhissāmi atthaṃ sūlāvuto puriso luddakammo paṇītadaṇḍo[^072-182] anusattarūpo.[^072-183]  || 581 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].67       Ettāvatā vīsatirattimattā yato āvuto[^072-184] n'; eva jīvati na mato tāhaṃ mocayissāmi dāni yathāmati[^072-185] anujānātu saṅgho.  || 582 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].68       Etañ ca aññañ ca lahuṃ pamuñca ko taṃ vadetha[^072-186] tathā karontaṃ yathā pajānāsi tathā karohi yathāmati[^072-185] anujānāti saṅgho.  || 583 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].69      So taṃ padesaṃ upasaṅkamitvā sūlāvutaṃ mocayi khippam eva, mā bhāyi sammā ti[^072-187] ca[^072-187] taṃ avoca tikicchakānañ ca upaṭṭhapesi.  || 584 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].70       Kappitakañ[^072-170] ca upasaṅkamitvā ten'; eva[^072-188] saha saṃvibhajitva[^072-188] kāle sayam mukhen'; upanisajja[^072-173] Licchavi tath'; eva[^072-189] pucchittha naṃ[^072-189] kāraṇatthiko.  || 585 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].71       Sūlāvuto puriso luddakammo paṇītadaṇḍo[^072-182] anusattarūpo[^072-183] ettāvatā vīsatirattimattā yato āvuto[^072-184] n'; eva jīvati na mato,  || 586 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].72       So mocito[^072-190] gantvā mayā idāni etassa yakkhassa vaco hi bhante siyā nu kho kāraṇaṃ kiñcid eva yena so nirayaṃ no vajeyya.  || 587 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].73       Ācikkha bhante yadi atthi hetu saddhāyitaṃ[^072-80] hetuvaco[^072-191] suṇoma na tesaṃ kammānaṃ vināsam atthi avedayitvā idha vyantibhāvo.  ||  
588 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 073 -->
<a id="page-073"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 073]*

Vv_IV,1[=36].74       Sace sa[^073-192] dhammāni[^073-193] samācareyya sakkacca[^073-194] rattindivam appamatto mucceyya[^073-195] so nirayā ca[^073-196] tamhā kammaṃ siyā aññatra vedanīyaṃ.  || 589 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].75       Aññāto[^073-197] eso purisassa attho mamaṃ pi[^073-198] dāni[^073-198] anukampa bhante anusāsa maṃ ovada bhūripañña yathā ahaṃ no[^073-199] nirayaṃ vajeyyaṃ.  || 590 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].76       Ajj'; eva buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ upehi dhammañ ca saṅghañ ca pasannacitto tath'; eva sikkhāya[^073-200] padāni[^073-200] pañca akhaṇḍaphullāni samādiyassu.[^073-201]  || 591 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].77       Pāṇātipātā viramassu khippaṃ loke adinnaṃ parivajjayassu amajjapo mā ca musā abhāṇi[^073-202] sakena dārena ca hohi[^073-203] tuṭṭho; imañ ca ariyaṃ[^073-204] aṭṭhaṅgavaren'; upetaṃ[^073-204] samādiyāhi kusalaṃ sukhudrayaṃ.[^073-205]  || 592 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].78   Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañ ca paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ annaṃ[^073-206] pānaṃ khādanīyaṃ vatthasenāsanāni[^073-207] ca dadāhi[^073-208] ujubhūtesu vippasannena[^073-209] cetasā.[^073-209]  || 593 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].79   Bhikkhū ca[^073-210] sīlasampanne vītarāge bahussute tappehi[^073-211] annapānena sadā puññaṃ pavaḍḍhati.  || 594 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].80       Evañ ca dhammāni[^073-193] samācaranto sakkacca[^073-194] rattindivam appamatto mucceyya[^073-212] so tvaṃ[^073-213] nirayā ca[^073-214] tamhā kammaṃ siyā aññatra vedanīyaṃ.  || 595 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].81       Ajj'; eva buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ upemi dhammañ ca saṅghañ ca pasannacitto tath'; eva sikkhāya[^073-200] padāni[^073-200] pañca akhaṇḍaphullāni samādiyāmi.[^073-215]  || 596 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].82       Pāṇātipātā viramāmi khippaṃ loke adinnaṃ parivajjayāmi amajjapo no ca musā bhaṇāmi sakena dārena ca homi tuṭṭho imañ ca ariyaṃ[^073-204] aṭṭhaṅgavaren'; upetaṃ[^073-204] samādiyāmi kusalaṃ sukhudrayaṃ.[^073-205]  || 597 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].83   Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañ ca paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ annaṃ[^073-206] pānaṃ khādanīyaṃ vatthasenāsanāni[^073-207] ca,  ||  
598 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 074 -->
<a id="page-074"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 074]*

Vv_IV,1[=36].84   Bhikkhū ca sīlasampanne vītarāge bahussute dadāmi na vikappāmi[^074-216] buddhānaṃ sāsane rato.  || 599 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].85       Etādiso Licchavi Ambasakkharo[^074-3] Vesāliyaṃ aññataro upāsako saddho mudū[^074-217] kārakaro ca bhikkhusaṅghañ ca sakkacca tadā upaṭṭhahi.  || 600 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].86       Sūlāvuto ca arogo hutvā serī[^074-218] sukhī[^074-218] pabbajjaṃ upāgami bhikkhuñ ca[^074-219] āgamma Kappitakuttamaṃ[^074-170] ubho pi sāmaññaphalāni ajjhaguṃ.  || 601 ||  
Vv_IV,1[=36].87       Etādisā sappurisāna[^074-220] sevanā mahapphalā[^074-221] hoti sataṃ vijānataṃ sūlāvuto aggaphalaṃ aphassayi[^074-222] phalaṃ kaniṭṭhaṃ pana Ambasakkharo[^074-3] ti.  || 602 ||  
Ambasakkharapetavatthu paṭhamaṃ

37 Serissakapetavatthu Vv_IV,2[=37].1-54 See Vimānavatthu No. [^079-84].  ||

Serissakapetavatthu dutiyaṃ BHĀṆAVĀRAṂ TATIYAṂ 38 Nandakapetavetthu Vv_IV,3[=38].1    Rājā Piṅgalako nāma Suraṭṭhānaṃ adhipati ahu Moriyānaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā Suraṭṭhaṃ punarāgamā.  || 657 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].2    Uṇhe majjhantike[^079-1] kāle rājā vaṅkaṃ[^079-2] upāgami addasa maggaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ petānaṃ vaṇṇanāpathaṃ.[^079-3]  || 658 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].3    Sārathiṃ āmantayī[^079-4] rājā: ayaṃ maggo ramaṇīyo khemo sovatthiko sivo iminā[^079-5] sārathi yāhi[^079-6] Suraṭṭhānaṃ santike ito.  || 659 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].4    Tena[^079-7] pāyāsi Soraṭṭho senāya caturaṅginiyā ubbiggarūpo puriso Soraṭṭhaṃ[^079-8] etad abravi:[^079-9]  || 660 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].5    Kummaggaṃ[^079-10] paṭipann'; amhā bhiṃsanaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ purato dissati[^079-11] maggo pacchato co na dissati.  || 661 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].6    Kummaggaṃ[^079-10] paṭipann'; amhā Yamapurisāna[^079-12] santike amānuso vāyati gandho ghoso sūyati[^079-13] dāruṇo.  || 662 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].7    Saṃviggo rājā Soraṭṭho[^079-14] sārathiṃ etad abravi:[^079-9] kummaggaṃ[^079-10] paṭipann'; amhā bhiṃsanaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ purato[^079-15] dissati maggo pacchato ca na dissati.  || 663 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].8    Kummaggaṃ[^079-10] paṭipann'; amhā Yamapurisāna[^079-12] santike amānuso vāyati gandho ghoso sūyati[^079-13] dāruṇo.  || 664 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].9    Hatthikkhandhañ ca[^079-16] āruyha[^079-16] olokento catuddisaṃ[^079-17] addasa nigrodhaṃ[^079-18] ramaṇīyaṃ[^079-18] pādapaṃ chāyāsampannaṃ.[^079-19] nīlabbhavaṇṇasadisaṃ meghavaṇṇasirīnibhaṃ.[^079-20]  ||  
665 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 080 -->
<a id="page-080"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 080]*

Vv_IV,3[=38].10   Sārathiṃ āmantayī[^080-21] rājā, kiṃ eso dissati brahā nīlabbhavaṇṇasadiso meghavaṇṇasirīnibho.[^080-20]  || 666 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].11   Nigrodho[^080-22] so[^080-22] mahārāja pādapo chāyāsampanno[^080-19] nīlabbhavaṇṇasadiso meghavaṇṇasirīnibho.[^080-20]  || 667 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].12   Tena pāyāsi Soraṭṭho[^080-14] yena so dissati[^080-23] brahā nīlabbhavaṇṇasadiso meghavaṇṇasirīnibho.[^080-20]  || 668 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].13   Hatthikkhandhato oruyha rājā rukkhaṃ upāgami nisīdi rukkhamūlasmiṃ sāmacco saparijjano,[^080-24] pūraṃ pānīyakarakaṃ[^080-25] pūve citte[^080-26] ca ca addasa.  || 669 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].14   Puriso ca[^080-27] devavaṇṇī[^080-27] sabbābharaṇabhūsito upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ Soraṭṭhaṃ[^080-8] etad abravī:[^080-9]  || 670 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].15   Svāgataṃ te mahārāja atho te adurāgataṃ pivatu devo pānīyaṃ pūve khāda arindama.  || 671 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].16   Pivitvā rājā pānīyaṃ sāmacco saparijjano[^080-24] pūve khāditvā pītvā[^080-28] ca Soraṭṭho[^080-14] etad abravī:[^080-9]  || 672 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].17   Devatā nu 'si gandhabbo ādu Sakko purindado ajānantā[^080-29] taṃ pucchāma kathaṃ jānemu taṃ mayaṃ.  || 673 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].18   N'; amhi[^080-30] devo na gandhabbo nāpi[^080-31] Sakko purindado peto ahaṃ mahārāja Suraṭṭhā[^080-32] idha-m-āgato.  || 674 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].19   Kiṃsīlo kiṃsamācāro Suraṭṭhasmiṃ pure tuvaṃ kena te brahmacariyena ānubhāvo ayaṃ tava.  || 675 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].20   Taṃ suṇohi mahārāja[^080-33] arindama raṭṭhavaḍḍhana[^080-34] amaccā pārisajjā ca brāhmaṇo ca purohito.  || 676 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].21   Suraṭṭhasmā[^080-35] ahaṃ deva puriso pāpacetaso micchādiṭṭhi ca dussīlo kadariyo paribhāsako.  || 677 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].22   Dadantānaṃ karontānaṃ vārayissaṃ bahujjanaṃ[^080-36] aññesaṃ dadamānānaṃ antarāyakaro[^080-37] ahaṃ:[^080-37]  || 678 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].23   Vipāko natthi dānassa saṃyamassa kuto phalaṃ natthi ācariyo nāma adantaṃ ko damessati.[^080-38]  || 679 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].24   Samatulyāni bhūtāni kuto[^080-39] jeṭṭhāpacāyiko natthi balaṃ viriyaṃ[^080-40] vā kuto uṭṭhānaporisaṃ.  || 680 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].25   Natthi dānaphalaṃ nāma na visodheti verinaṃ laddheyyaṃ labhate macco niyatipariṇāmajā.[^080-41]  ||  
681 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 081 -->
<a id="page-081"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 081]*

Vv_IV,3[=38].26   Natthi mātā pitā bhātā loko natthi ito paraṃ natthi dinnaṃ natthi hutaṃ sunihitam pi[^081-42] na vijjati.  || 682 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].27   Yo pi haneyya[^081-43] purisaṃ parassa[^081-44] chindate[^081-45] siraṃ na koci kiñci[^081-46] hanati sattannaṃ vivaram antare.  || 683 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].28   Acchejjabhejjo[^081-47] jīvo aṭṭhaṃso guḷaparimaṇḍalo[^081-48] yojanānaṃ[^081-49] sataṃ[^081-50] pañca ko jīvaṃ chettum[^081-51] arahati.  || 684 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].29   Yathā suttaguḷe[^081-52] khitte nibbeṭhentaṃ[^081-53] palāyati evam evam pi[^081-54] so jīvo nibbeṭhento[^081-53] palāyati.  || 685 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].30   Yathā gāmato nikkhamma aññaṃ gāmaṃ pavisati[^081-55] evam evam pi[^081-56] so jīvo aññaṃ kāyaṃ[^081-57] pavisati.[^081-55]  || 686 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].31   Yathā gehato nikkhamma aññaṃ gehaṃ pavisati[^081-55] evam evam pi so jīvo aññaṃ bondiṃ pavisati.[^081-55]  || 687 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].32   Cūḷāsīti[^081-58] mahākappuno[^081-59] satasahassāni pi[^081-60] hi[^081-61] ye[^081-62] bālā ye ca paṇḍitā saṃsāraṃ khepayitvāna dukkhass'; antaṃ karissare.  || 688 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].33   Mitāni sukhadukkhāni doṇehi piṭakehi ca jino sabbaṃ pajānāti sammūḷhā[^081-63] itarā pajā.  || 689 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].34   Evaṃdiṭṭhi pure āsiṃ sammūḷho[^081-63] mohapāruto micchādiṭṭhi ca dussīlo kadariyo paribhāsako.  || 690 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].35   Oraṃ me chahi māsehi kālakiriyā[^081-64] bhavissati, ekantaṃ[^081-65] kaṭukaṃ ghoraṃ nirayaṃ papatiss'; ahaṃ.[^081-66]  || 691 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].36   Catukkaṇṇaṃ catudvāraṃ vibhattaṃ bhāgaso mitaṃ ayopākārapariyantaṃ ayasā paṭikujjitaṃ.  || 692 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].37   Tassa ayomayā bhūmi jalitā tejasā yutā, samantā yojanasataṃ pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā.  || 693 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].38  Vassāni[^081-67] satasahassāni ghoso sūyati[^081-13] tāvade, lakkho eso mahārāja satabhāgavassakoṭiyo[^081-68] koṭisatasahassāni niraye paccare janā --  || 694 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].39   Micchādiṭṭhī ca[^081-69] dussīlā ye ca ariyūpavādino tatthāhaṃ dīgham addhānaṃ dukkhaṃ vedissa[^081-70] vedanaṃ, phalaṃ pāpassa kammassa tasmā socām'; ahaṃ bhusaṃ.  ||  
695 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 082 -->
<a id="page-082"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 082]*

Vv_IV,3[=38].40   Taṃ suṇohi mahārāja arindama raṭṭhavaḍḍhana[^082-34] dhītā mayhaṃ mahārāja Uttarā bhaddam atthu te.  || 696 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].41   Karoti bhaddakaṃ kammaṃ sīles '; uposathe[^082-71] ratā saññatā[^082-72] saṃvibhāgī ca vadaññū[^082-73] vītamaccharā;[^082-74]  || 697 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].42   Akhaṇḍakārī sikkhāya[^082-75] suṇhā parakulesu ca upāsikā Sakyamunino sambuddhassa sirīmato.  || 698 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].43   Bhikkhu ca sīlasampanno gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi okkhittacakkhu[^082-76] satimā guttadvāro susaṃvuto.  || 699 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].44   Sapadānaṃ caramāno agamā taṃ nivesanaṃ tam addasa mahārāja Uttarā bhaddam atthu te.  || 700 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].45   Pūraṃ pānīyassa[^082-77] karakaṃ pūve citte ca sā adā: pitā me kālakato[^082-78] bhante tass'; etaṃ[^082-79] upakappatu.[^082-79]  || 701 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].46   Samanantarānuddiṭṭhe[^082-80] vipāko upapajjatha[^082-81] bhuñjāmi kāmakāmī 'haṃ[^082-82] rājā Vessavaṇo yathā.  || 702 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].47   Taṃ suṇohi mahārāja arindama raṭṭhavaḍḍhana[^082-34] sadevakassa lokassa buddho aggo pavuccati, taṃ buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gaccha saputtadāro[^082-83] arindama.  || 703 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].48   Aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena phusanti amataṃ padaṃ taṃ dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gaccha saputtadāro[^082-83] arindama.  || 704 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].49   Cattāro maggapaṭipannā[^082-84] cattāro ca phale ṭhitā esa saṅgho ujubhūto paññāsīlasamāhito, taṃ saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gaccha saputtadāro arindama.  || 705 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].50       Pāṇātipātā viramassu khippaṃ loke adinnaṃ parivajjayassu amajjapo[^082-85] mā[^082-86] ca musā abhāṇi[^082-87] sakena dārena ca hohi tuṭṭho.  || 706 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].51   Atthakāmo 'si me yakkha hitakāmo 'si devate karomi tuyhaṃ vacanaṃ tvaṃ si[^082-88] ācariyo mama.  || 707 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].52   Upemi saraṇaṃ buddhaṃ dhammañ cāpi anuttaraṃ saṅghañ ca naradevassa gacchāmi saraṇaṃ ahaṃ.  || 708 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].53       Pāṇātipātā viramāmi khippaṃ loke adinnaṃ parivajjayāmi amajjapo no ca musā bhaṇāmi sakena dārena ca[^082-89] homi tuṭṭho  ||  
709 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 083 -->
<a id="page-083"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 083]*

Vv_IV,3[=38].54   Opuṇāmi[^083-90] mahāvāte[^083-4] nadiyā vā[^083-91] sīghagāmiyā[^083-92] vamāmi[^083-93] pāpakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ buddhānaṃ sāsane rato.  || 710 ||  
Vv_IV,3[=38].55   Idaṃ vatvāna Soraṭṭho[^083-14] viramitvā pāpadassanā[^083-94] namo bhagavato katvā pāmokkho ratham āruhī[^083-95] ti.  || 711 ||  
Nandakapetavatthu[^083-96] tatiyaṃ

39 Revatīpetavatthu Vv_IV,4[=39].1-25 See Vimānavatthu No. 52.  ||  
712-736 ||  
Revatīpetavatthu catutthaṃ 40 Ucchupetavatthu Vv_IV,5[=40].1        Idaṃ mama[^085-1] ucchuvanaṃ mahantaṃ nibbattati puññaphalaṃ anappakaṃ taṃ[^085-2] dāni me paribhogaṃ na upeti[^085-2] ācikkha bhante kissa ayaṃ vipāko.  || 737 ||  
Vv_IV,5[=40].2        Haññāmi[^085-3] khajjāmi ca vāyamāmi[^085-4] parisakkāmi paribhuñjituṃ kiñci svāhaṃ chinnathāmo[^085-5] kapaṇo lālapāmi[^085-6] kissa[^085-7] kammassa ayaṃ vipāko.  || 738 ||  
Vv_IV,5[=40].3        Vighāto cāhaṃ paripatāmi chamāyaṃ parivattāmi vāricaro va ghamme rudato[^085-8] ca me assukā niggalanti[^085-8] ācikkha bhante kissa ayaṃ vipāko.  || 739 ||  
Vv_IV,5[=40].4        Chāto kilanto ca pipāsito ca santasito[^085-9] sātasukhaṃ na vinde pucchāmi taṃ etam atthaṃ bhadante[^085-10] kathan nu ucchuparibhogaṃ labheyyaṃ.  || 740 ||  
Vv_IV,5[=40].5        Pure tuvaṃ kammam akāsi attanā manussabhūto purimāya jātiyā ahañ ca taṃ etam atthaṃ vadāmi sutvāna tvaṃ etam atthaṃ vijāna.[^085-11]  ||  
741 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 086 -->
<a id="page-086"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 086]*

Vv_IV,5[=40].6        Ucchuṃ[^086-12] tvaṃ[^086-13] khādamāno payāto puriso ca[^086-14] te piṭṭhito anvagacchi[^086-15] so ca taṃ paccāsanto kathesi tassa tuvaṃ na kiñ ci ālapittha.  || 742 ||  
Vv_IV,5[=40].7        So ca taṃ abhaṇantaṃ[^086-16] ayāci[^086-17] deh'; ayya[^086-18] ucchun ti ca taṃ avoca tassa tuvaṃ piṭṭhito ucchuṃ adāsi tass'; etaṃ kammassa ayaṃ vipāko.  || 743 ||  
Vv_IV,5[=40].8        Iṅgha tuvaṃ[^086-19] piṭṭhito[^086-19] gaṇha[^086-20] ucchuṃ[^086-20] gahetvāna[^086-21] khādassu yāvad atthaṃ ten'; eva tvaṃ attamano bhavissasi haṭṭho udaggo[^086-22] ca pamodito ca.  || 744 ||  
Vv_IV,5[=40].9        Gantvāna so piṭṭhito aggahesi gahetvāna taṃ khādi yāvad atthaṃ ten'; eva so attamano ahosi haṭṭho udaggo[^086-22] ca pamodito cā[^086-23] ti.  || 745 ||  
Ucchupetavatthu pañcamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 087 -->
<a id="page-087"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 087]*

41 Kumārapetavatthu Vv_IV,6[=41].1    Sāvatthi[^087-1] nāma nagaraṃ Himavantassa passato tattha[^087-2] āsuṃ[^087-2] dve kumārā[^087-3] rājaputtā ti me sutaṃ.  || 746 ||  
Vv_IV,6[=41].2    Sammattā[^087-4] rajanīyesu[^087-5] kāmassādābhinandino paccuppanne[^087-6] sukhe[^087-6] giddhā na te passiṃsu 'nāgataṃ.  || 747 ||  
Vv_IV,6[=41].3    Te cutā[^087-7] ca manussattā paralokaṃ ito gatā te 'dha[^087-8] {ghosenty'; adissantā[^087-8]} pubbe dukkaṭam attano:  || 748 ||  
Vv_IV,6[=41].4    Bahūsu[^087-9] vata[^087-9] santesu deyyadhamme upaṭṭhite nāsakkhimhā ca attānaṃ parittaṃ[^087-10] kātuṃ sukhāvahaṃ,  || 749 ||  
Vv_IV,6[=41].5    Kiṃ tato pāpakaṃ assa yaṃ[^087-11] no[^087-11] rājakulā cutā upapannā[^087-12] pettivisayaṃ[^087-13] khuppipāsasamappitā.[^087-14]  || 750 ||  
Vv_IV,6[=41].6    Sāmino idha hutvāna honti assāmino[^087-15] tahiṃ caranti[^087-16] khuppipāsāya manussā unnatonatā.[^087-17]  || 751 ||  
Vv_IV,6[=41].7    Etam[^087-18] ādīnavaṃ ñatvā issaramadasambhavaṃ pahāya issaramadaṃ[^087-19] bhave saggagato naro kāyassa bhedā sappañño saggaṃ so upapajjatī ti.  || 752 ||  
Kumārapetavatthu chaṭṭhaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 088 -->
<a id="page-088"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 088]*

42 Rājaputtapetavatthu Vv_IV,7[=42].1    Pubbe katānaṃ kammānaṃ vipāko mathaye manaṃ; rūpe sadde rase gandhe phoṭṭhabbe[^088-1] ca manorame,  || 753 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].2    Naccaṃ gītaṃ ratiṃ khiḍḍaṃ anubhutvā anappakaṃ uyyāne paricaritvā[^088-2] pavisanto Giribbajaṃ,  || 754 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].3    Isiṃ Sunettam addakkhi attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ appicchaṃ hirisampannaṃ[^088-3] uñche pattagate[^088-4] rataṃ.  || 755 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].4    Hatthikkhandhato oruyha laddhā bhante ti c'; abravi;[^088-5] tassa pattaṃ gahetvāna uccaṃ paggayha khattiyo,  || 756 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].5    Thaṇḍile[^088-6] pattaṃ bhinditvā hasamāno apakkami, rañño Kitavass'; ahaṃ[^088-7] putto kiṃ maṃ bhikkhu karissasi.  || 757 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].6    Tassa kammassa pharusassa vipāko kaṭuko ahu yaṃ rājaputto vedesi nirayamhi samappito.  || 758 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].7    {Chaḷ'; eva} caturāsīti vassāni nahutāni[^088-8] ca bhusaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchittho[^088-9] niraye katakibbiso.  || 759 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].8    Uttāno pi ca paccittha nikujjo vāmadakkhiṇo uddhaṃpādo ṭhito c'; eva ciraṃ bālo apaccatha.[^088-10]  || 760 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].9    Bahūni vassasahassāni pūgāni nahutāni ca bhusaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchittho[^088-9] niraye katakibbiso.  || 761 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].10   Etādisaṃ kho kaṭukaṃ appaduṭṭhapadosinaṃ paccanti pāpakammantā isim āsajja subbataṃ.  || 762 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].11   So tattha bahudukkhāni vedayitvā bahuṃ[^088-11] dukhaṃ[^088-11] khuppipāsāhato[^088-12] nāma peto āsi tato cuto.  || 763 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].12   Etaṃ[^088-13] ādīnavaṃ disvā[^088-14] issaramadasambhavaṃ pahāya issaramadaṃ nivātam anuvattaye.  || 764 ||  
Vv_IV,7[=42].13   Diṭṭhe va dhamme pāsaṃso yo[^088-15] buddhesu sagāravo kāyassa bhedā sappañño saggaṃ so upapajjatī ti.  || 765 ||  
Rājaputtapetavatthu sattamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 089 -->
<a id="page-089"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 089]*

43 Gūthakhādakapetavatthu Vv_IV,8[=43].1    Gūthakūpato uggantvā ko nu dīno[^089-1] patiṭṭhasi[^089-1] nissaṃsayaṃ pāpakammanto kin nu saddahase[^089-2] tuvaṃ.  || 766 ||  
Vv_IV,8[=43].2    Ahaṃ bhadente[^089-3] peto 'mhi duggato Yamalokiko pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokam ito gato.  || 767 ||  
Vv_IV,8[=43].3    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kaṭaṃ kissa kammavipākena idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasi.  || 768 ||  
Vv_IV,8[=43].4    Ahu āvāsiko mayhaṃ issukī kulamaccharī ajjhosito[^089-4] mayhaṃ ghare kadariyo paribhāsako.  || 769 ||  
Vv_IV,8[=43].5    Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā bhikkhavo paribhāsisaṃ[^089-5] tassa kammavipākena petalokam ito gato.  || 770 ||  
Vv_IV,8[=43].6    Amitto mittavaṇṇena yo te āsi kulūpako[^089-6] kāyassa bhedā duppañño kin nu pecca gatiṃ gato.  || 771 ||  
Vv_IV,8[=43].7    Tass'; evāhaṃ pāpakammassa sīse tiṭṭhāmi matthake so ca paravisayaṃ patto mam'; eva paricārako.[^089-7]  || 772 ||  
Vv_IV,8[=43].8    Yaṃ bhadante[^089-8] hadant'; aññe[^089-9] etaṃ me hoti bhojanaṃ ahañ ca kho yaṃ hadāmi[^089-10] etaṃ so upajīvati.  || 773 ||  
Gūthakhādakapetavatthu aṭṭhamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 090 -->
<a id="page-090"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 090]*

44 Gūthakhādakapetavatthu[^090-1] Vv_IV,9[=44].1    Gūthakūpato uggantvā kā nu dīnā[^090-2] patiṭṭhasi[^090-2] nissaṃsayaṃ pāpakammantā kin nu saddahase tuvaṃ.  || 774 ||  
Vv_IV,9[=44].2    Ahaṃ bhadante petī 'mhi duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokam ito gatā.  || 775 ||  
Vv_IV,9[=44].3    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kaṭaṃ kissa kammavipākena idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasi.  || 776 ||  
Vv_IV,9[=44].4    Ahu āvāsiko mayhaṃ issukī kulamaccharī ajjhosito mayhaṃ ghare kadariyo paribhāsako.  || 777 ||  
Vv_IV,9[=44].5    Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā bhikkhavo paribhāsisaṃ tassa kammavipākena petalokam ito gatā.  || 778 ||  
Vv_IV,9[=44].6    Amitto mittavaṇṇena yo te āsi kulūpako kāyassa bhedā duppañño kin nu pecca gatiṃ gato.  || 779 ||  
Vv_IV,9[=44].7    Tass'; evāhaṃ pāpakammassa sīse tiṭṭhāmi matthake so ca paravisayaṃ patto mam'; eva paricārako.  || 780 ||  
Vv_IV,9[=44].8    Yaṃ bhadante hadant'; aññe etaṃ me hoti bhojanaṃ ahañ ca kho yaṃ hadāmi etaṃ so upajīvati.  || 781 ||  
Gūthakhādakapetavatthu[^090-1] navamaṃ &1 45 Gaṇapetavatthu Vv_IV,10[=45].1    Naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāttha[^090-1] kisā[^090-2] dhamanisanthatā[^090-3] upphāsulikā kisikā[^090-4] ke nu tumhe 'ttha[^090-5] mārisā.  || 782 ||  
Vv_IV,10[=45].2    Mayaṃ bhadante[^090-6] pet'; amhā[^090-7] duggatā Yamalokikā pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokam ito gatā.  ||  
783 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 091 -->
<a id="page-091"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 091]*

Vv_IV,10[=45].3    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ kissa kammavipākena idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasi.  || 784 ||  
Vv_IV,10[=45].4    Anāvaṭesu[^091-8] titthesu vicinimh'; addhamāsakaṃ[^091-9] santesu deyyadhammesu dīpaṃ nākamha attano.[^091-10]  || 785 ||  
Vv_IV,10[=45].5    Nadiṃ upema tasitā rittakā parivattati chāyaṃ upema uṇhesu ātapo parivattati.  || 786 ||  
Vv_IV,10[=45].6    Aggivaṇṇo ca[^091-11] no vāto dahanto[^091-12] upavāyati etañ ca bhante arahāma aññañ ca pāpakaṃ tato.  || 787 ||  
Vv_IV,10[=45].7    Api[^091-13] yojanāni gacchāma chātā āhāragedhino[^091-14] aladdhā va[^091-15] nivattāma[^091-16] aho no appapuññatā.  || 788 ||  
Vv_IV,10[=45].8    Chātā pamucchitā bhantā[^091-17] bhūmiyaṃ paṭisumbhitā uttānā patikirāma[^091-18] avakujjā patāmase.  || 789 ||  
Vv_IV,10[=45].9    Te ca tatth'; eva patitā bhūmiyaṃ paṭisumbhitā uraṃ sīsañ ca ghaṭṭema aho no appapuññatā.  || 790 ||  
Vv_IV,10[=45].10   Etañ ca bhante arahāma aññañ ca pāpakaṃ tato santesu deyyadhammesu dīpaṃ nākamha attano.[^091-10]  || 791 ||  
Vv_IV,10[=45].11   Te hi nūna ito gantvā yoniṃ laddhāna mānusiṃ vadaññū sīlasampannā kāhāma kusalaṃ bahun ti.  || 792 ||  
Gaṇapetavatthu dasamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 092 -->
<a id="page-092"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 092]*

46 Pāṭaliputtapetavatthu Vv_IV,11[=46].1        Diṭṭhā tayā nirayā tiracchānayonī[^092-1] petā asurā athavā pi manussā[^092-2] devā sayam addasa kammavipākam attano nessāmi taṃ Pāṭaliputtam akkhataṃ.  || 793 ||  
Vv_IV,11[=46].2    Atthakāmo 'si me yakkha hitakāmo 'si devate karomi tuyhaṃ vacanaṃ tvam asi ācariyo mama[^092-3]  || 794 ||  
Vv_IV,11[=46].3        Diṭṭhā mayā nirayā tiracchānayonī[^092-1] petā asurā athavā pi manussā[^092-2] devā sayam addasaṃ[^092-4] kammavipākam attano kāhāmi puññāni anappakānī ti.  || 795 ||  
Pāṭaliputtapetavatthu ekādasamaṃ &1 47 Ambapetavatthu Vv_IV,12[=47].1        Ayañ ca te pokkharaṇī surammā samā suppatitthā[^092-1] ca mahodakā[^092-2] ca supupphitā bhamaragaṇānukiṇṇā kathaṃ tayā laddhā ayaṃ manuññā.  || 796 ||  
Vv_IV,12[=47].2        Idañ ca te ambavanaṃ surammaṃ sabbotukaṃ dhārayate[^092-3] phalāni supupphitaṃ bhamaragaṇānukiṇṇaṃ kathaṃ tayā laddham idaṃ vimānaṃ.  || 797 ||  
Vv_IV,12[=47].3    Ambapakkodakaṃ[^092-4] yāgu[^092-5] sītacchāyā manoramā dhītāya dinnadānena tena me idha labbhati.  || 798 ||  
Vv_IV,12[=47].4       Sandiṭṭhikaṃ[^092-6] kammaṃ[^092-7] evaṃ[^092-8] passatha dānassa damassa saṃyamassa vipākaṃ dāsī ahaṃ ayyakulesu hutvā suṇisā homi agārassa ca[^092-9] issarā ti.[^092-10]  || 799 ||  
Ambapetavatthu[^092-11] dvādasamaṃ &2


<!-- PTS Page 093 -->
<a id="page-093"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 093]*

48 Akkharukkhapetavatthu [^093-1]Yaṃ dadāti na taṃ hoti deth'; eva dānaṃ datvā[^093-1] ubhayaṃ tarati ubhayaṃ tena dānena[^093-2] gacchati jāgaratha mā pamajjathā ti.  || 800 ||  
Akkharukkhapetavatthu[^093-3] terasamaṃ &1 49 Bhogasaṃharapetavatthu Vv_IV,14[=49].1    Mayaṃ bhoge saṃharimha[^093-1] samena visamena ca te aññe paribhuñjanti mayaṃ dukkhassa bhāginī ti.  || 801 ||  
Bhogasaṃharapetavatthu cuddasamaṃ &2 50 Seṭṭhiputtapetavatthu Vv_IV,15[=50].1    Saṭṭhivassasahassāni paripuṇṇāni sabbaso niraye paccamānānaṃ kadā anto bhavissati.  || 802 ||  
Vv_IV,15[=50].2    Natthi anto kuto anto na anto patidissati[^093-1] tathā hi pakataṃ pāpaṃ mama[^093-2] tuyhañ ca mārisa.[^093-2]  ||  
803 ||  


<!-- PTS Page 094 -->
<a id="page-094"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 094]*

Vv_IV,15[=50].3    Dujjīvitam ajīvamha[^094-3] ye[^094-4] sante na dadamhase[^094-4] santesu deyyadhammesu dīpaṃ nākamha attano.  || 804 ||  
Vv_IV,15[=50].4    So hi[^094-5] nūna ito gantvā yoniṃ laddhāna mānusiṃ vadaññū sīlasampanno kāhāmi kusalaṃ bahun ti.  || 805 ||  
Seṭṭhiputtapetavatthu pannarasamaṃ &1 51 Saṭṭhikūṭasahassapetavatthu Vv_IV,16[=51].1    Kin nu ummattarūpo va[^094-1] migo bhanto va dhāvasi nissaṃsayaṃ pāpakammanto[^094-2] kin nu saddāyase[^094-3] tuvaṃ.  || 806 ||  
Vv_IV,16[=51].2    Ahaṃ bhadante[^094-4] peto 'mhi duggato Yamalokiko pāpakammaṃ karitvāna petalokam ito gato.  || 807 ||  
Vv_IV,16[=51].3    Saṭṭhikūṭasahassāni paripuṇṇāni sabbaso sīse mayhaṃ nipatanti te bhindanti ca matthakaṃ.  || 808 ||  
Vv_IV,16[=51].4    Kin nu kāyena vācāya manasā dukkaṭaṃ kaṭaṃ kissa kammacipākena idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasi.  || 809 ||  
Vv_IV,16[=51].5    Saṭṭhikūṭasahassāni paripuṇṇāni sabbaso sīse tuyhaṃ nipatanti te bhindanti ca matthakaṃ.  || 810 ||  
Vv_IV,16[=51].6    Ath'; addasāsim[^094-5] sambuddhaṃ Sunettaṃ bhāvitindriyaṃ nisinnaṃ rukkhamūlasmiṃ jhāyantam akutobhayaṃ.  || 811 ||  
Vv_IV,16[=51].7    Sālittakappahārena bhindissaṃ[^094-6] tassa matthakaṃ tassa kammavipākena idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchisaṃ.[^094-7]  || 812 ||  
Vv_IV,16[=51].8    Saṭṭhikūṭasahassāni paripuṇṇāni sabbaso sīse mayhaṃ nipatanti te bhindanti ca matthakaṃ.  || 813 ||  
Vv_IV,16[=51].9    Dhammena te kāpurisa, saṭṭhikūṭasahassāni paripuṇṇāni sabbaso sīse tuyham nipatanti te bhindanti ca matthakan ti.  || 814 ||  
Saṭṭhikūṭasahassapetavatthu soḷasamaṃ BHĀṆAVĀRAṂ CATUTTHAṂ


<!-- PTS Page 095 -->
<a id="page-095"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 095]*

Tass'; uddānaṃ:[^095-8] Ambasakkharo Serīsako Piṅgalo Revati-ucchukhādakā[^095-9] dve kumārā dve[^095-10] gūthā[^095-10] gaṇa-Pāṭali-pokkharañ ca[^095-11] akkharukkha[^095-12]-bhogasaṃharā[^095-13] seṭṭhiputta-sālittakā[^095-14] iti[^095-15] soḷasavatthūni[^095-15] vaggo tena pavaccatī ti. Petavatthu samattaṃ MAHĀVAGGO CATUTTHO
